78→ * -------------------------------------------------------------------------*/\n    79→typedef struct mm_cache_entry {\n    80→    char              *key;\n    81→    unsigned char     *payload;\n    82→    size_t             payload_len;\n    83→\n    84→    char              *content_type;        /* MIME type (optional)  */\n    85→    char              *etag;                /* Strong/Weak ETag      */\n    86→\n    87→    time_t             expiry_ts;           /* Epoch time            */\n    88→    uint64_t           last_access;         /* Monotonic counter     */\n    89→\n    90→    struct mm_cache_entry *prev, *next;     /* LRU list              */\n    91→} mm_cache_entry_t;\n    92→\n    93→typedef struct {\n    94→    /* Configuration */\n    95→    size_t   capacity_bytes;   /* Max RAM in bytes                    */\n    96→    size_t   max_entries;      /* Cap on element count                */\n    97→\n    98→    /* Internal state */\n    99→    size_t   current_bytes;\n   100→    size_t   entry_count;\n   101→    size_t   tbl_size;         /* Hash table size (power of two)      */\n   102→    mm_cache_entry_t **table;\n   103→\n   104→    mm_cache_entry_t *lru_head;/* MRU                                 */\n   105→    mm_cache_entry_t *lru_tail;/* LRU                                 */\n   106→\n   107→    /* Concurrency control */\n   108→    pthread_rwlock_t rwlock;\n   109→\n   110→    /* Metrics */\n   111→    uint64_t hits;\n   112→    uint64_t misses;\n   113→    uint64_t evictions;\n   114→} mm_cache_t;\n   115→\n   116→\n   117→/* Publicly exported metrics snapshot */\n   118→typedef struct {\n   119→    uint64_t hits;\n   120→    uint64_t misses;\n   121→    uint64_t evictions;\n   122→    size_t   entry_count;\n   123→    size_t   current_bytes;\n   124→} mm_cache_metrics_t;\n   125→\n   126→\n   127→/* ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   128→ * Forward declarations\n   129→ * -------------------------------------------------------------------------*/\n   130→static uint64_t mm_fnv1a_hash(const void *data, size_t len);\n   131→static int      mm_cache_grow_table(mm_cache_t *c, size_t new_size);\n   132→static void     mm_cache_lru_touch(mm_cache_t *c, mm_cache_entry_t *e);\n   133→static void     mm_cache_evict_if_needed(mm_cache_t *c, size_t add_bytes);\n   134→\n   135→\n   136→/* ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   137→ * API IMPLEMENTATION\n   138→ * -------------------------------------------------------------------------*/\n   139→\n   140→/*\n   141→ * mm_cache_init\n   142→ * ----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   143→ * Initializes a response cache instance.\n   144→ *\n   145→ * Arguments:\n   146→ *   c               – pointer to an allocated mm_cache_t\n   147→ *   max_entries     – upper bound on number of cache records\n   148→ *   capacity_bytes  – max memory usage for payloads + metadata\n   149→ *\n   150→ * Return:\n   151→ *   0 on success, errno-compatible error code otherwise.\n   152→ */\n   153→int mm_cache_init(mm_cache_t *c,\n   154→                  size_t max_entries,\n   155→                  size_t capacity_bytes)\n   156→{\n   157→    if (!c || max_entries == 0 || capacity_bytes == 0)\n   158→        return EINVAL;\n   159→\n   160→    memset(c, 0, sizeof(*c));\n   161→\n   162→    c->max_entries    = max_entries;\n   163→    c->capacity_bytes = capacity_bytes;\n   164→\n   165→    /* Initial hash table size: next power-of-two >= max_entries * 2          */\n   166→    size_t tbl_size = MM_CACHE_DEFAULT_TBL_SIZE;\n   167→    while (tbl_size < (max_entries << 1)) tbl_size <<= 1;\n   168→\n   169→    if (mm_cache_grow_table(c, tbl_size) != 0)\n   170→        return ENOMEM;\n   171→\n   172→    if (pthread_rwlock_init(&c->rwlock, NULL) != 0)\n   173→        return errno;\n   174→\n   175→    mm_log(LOG_INFO, \"Response cache initialized (ver=%s, max_entries=%zu, \"\n   176→                     \"capacity=%zu KiB)\",\n   177→                     MM_CACHE_MODULE_VERSION,\n   178→                     max_entries,\n   179→                     capacity_bytes >> 10);\n   180→    return 0;\n   181→}\n   182→\n   183→/*\n   184→ * mm_cache_destroy\n   185→ * ----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   186→ * Frees all memory associated with the cache.\n   187→ */\n   188→void mm_cache_destroy(mm_cache_t *c)\n   189→{\n   190→    if (!c) return;\n   191→\n   192→    pthread_rwlock_wrlock(&c->rwlock);\n   193→\n   194→    for (size_t i = 0; i < c->tbl_size; ++i) {\n   195→        mm_cache_entry_t *e = c->table[i];\n   196→        if (e) {\n   197→            free(e->key);\n   198→            free(e->payload);\n   199→            free(e->content_type);\n   200→            free(e->etag);\n   201→            free(e);\n   202→        }\n   203→    }\n   204→    free(c->table);\n   205→\n   206→    pthread_rwlock_unlock(&c->rwlock);\n   207→    pthread_rwlock_destroy(&c->rwlock);\n   208→    memset(c, 0, sizeof(*c));\n   209→}\n   210→\n   211→/*\n   212→ * mm_cache_put\n   213→ * ----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   214→ * Stores a response in the cache.\n   215→ *\n   216→ * The cache takes ownership of the payload data (it copies it internally).\n   217→ *\n   218→ * Arguments:\n   219→ *   key             – request hash key  (string)\n   220→ *   payload         – response body\n   221→ *   payload_len     – size of response body\n   222→ *   content_type    – MIME type (may be NULL)\n   223→ *   etag            – optional ETag header\n   224→ *   ttl_seconds     – time-to-live for this cache record\n   225→ *\n   226→ * Return:\n   227→ *   0 on success, errno code on failure\n   228→ */\n   229→int mm_cache_put(mm_cache_t *c,\n   230→                 const char *key,\n   231→                 const unsigned char *payload,\n   232→                 size_t payload_len,\n   233→                 const char *content_type,\n   234→                 const char *etag,\n   235→                 uint32_t ttl_seconds)\n   236→{\n   237→    if (!c || !key || !payload || payload_len == 0)\n   238→        return EINVAL;\n   239→\n   240→    if (strlen(key) > MM_CACHE_MAX_KEYLEN)\n   241→        return ENAMETOOLONG;\n   242→\n   243→    if (ttl_seconds < MM_CACHE_MIN_TTL)\n   244→        ttl_seconds = MM_CACHE_MIN_TTL;\n   245→\n   246→    /* Pre-allocate memory before acquiring write lock to minimize lock time */\n   247→    mm_cache_entry_t *new_entry = calloc(1, sizeof(*new_entry));\n   248→    if (!new_entry)\n   249→        return ENOMEM;\n   250→\n   251→    new_entry->key = strdup(key);\n   252→    if (!new_entry->key) { free(new_entry); return ENOMEM; }\n   253→\n   254→    new_entry->payload = malloc(payload_len);\n   255→    if (!new_entry->payload) { free(new_entry->key); free(new_entry); return ENOMEM; }\n   256→    memcpy(new_entry->payload, payload, payload_len);\n   257→    new_entry->payload_len = payload_len;\n   258→\n   259→    if (content_type) {\n   260→        if (strlen(content_type) > MM_CACHE_MAX_CONTENTTYPE) {\n   261→            free(new_entry->payload); free(new_entry->key); free(new_entry);\n   262→            return ENAMETOOLONG;\n   263→        }\n   264→        new_entry->content_type = strdup(content_type);\n   265→        if (!new_entry->content_type) goto oom;\n   266→    }\n   267→\n   268→    if (etag) {\n   269→        if (strlen(etag) > MM_CACHE_MAX_ETAG) {\n   270→            free(new_entry->content_type); free(new_entry->payload);\n   271→            free(new_entry->key); free(new_entry); return ENAMETOOLONG;\n   272→        }\n   273→        new_entry->etag = strdup(etag);\n   274→        if (!new_entry->etag) goto oom;\n   275→    }\n   276→\n   277→    new_entry->expiry_ts   = time(NULL) + ttl_seconds;\n   278→    new_entry->last_access = (uint64_t)clock();\n   279→\n   280→    /* Lock and insert */\n   281→    pthread_rwlock_wrlock(&c->rwlock);\n   282→\n   283→    /* Evict expired + ensure capacity before insertion */\n   284→    mm_cache_evict_if_needed(c, payload_len);\n   285→\n   286→    uint64_t hash = mm_fnv1a_hash(key, strlen(key));\n   287→    size_t idx = hash & (c->tbl_size - 1);\n   288→\n   289→    while (c->table[idx] && strcmp(c->table[idx]->key, key) != 0)\n   290→        idx = (idx + 1) & (c->tbl_size - 1);\n   291→\n   292→    mm_cache_entry_t *existing = c->table[idx];\n   293→    if (existing) {\n   294→        /* Replace existing entry */\n   295→        c->current_bytes -= existing->payload_len;\n   296→        free(existing->payload);\n   297→        existing->payload       = new_entry->payload;\n   298→        existing->payload_len   = new_entry->payload_len;\n   299→        existing->expiry_ts     = new_entry->expiry_ts;\n   300→        existing->last_access   = new_entry->last_access;\n   301→\n   302→        free(existing->content_type);\n   303→        existing->content_type  = new_entry->content_type;\n   304→\n   305→        free(existing->etag);\n   306→        existing->etag          = new_entry->etag;\n   307→\n   308→        /* Touch */\n   309→        mm_cache_lru_touch(c, existing);\n   310→\n   311→        free(new_entry->key); /* key identical */\n   312→        free(new_entry);      /* container freed */\n   313→    } else {\n   314→        /* Insert brand new entry */\n   315→        c->table[idx] = new_entry;\n   316→        c->entry_count++;\n   317→        c->current_bytes += payload_len;\n   318→\n   319→        /* Add to LRU head */\n   320→        new_entry->next = c->lru_head;\n   321→        if (c->lru_head)\n   322→            c->lru_head->prev = new_entry;\n   323→        c->lru_head = new_entry;\n   324→        if (!c->lru_tail)\n   325→            c->lru_tail = new_entry;\n   326→    }\n   327→\n   328→    pthread_rwlock_unlock(&c->rwlock);\n   329→    return 0;\n   330→\n   331→oom:\n   332→    free(new_entry->etag);\n   333→    free(new_entry->content_type);\n   334→    free(new_entry->payload);\n   335→    free(new_entry->key);\n   336→    free(new_entry);\n   337→    return ENOMEM;\n   338→}\n   339→\n   340→/*\n   341→ * mm_cache_get\n   342→ * ----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   343→ * Retrieves a cached response. Ownership of *payload_out is transferred to\n   344→ * the caller (caller must free). content_type_out & etag_out point to\n   345→ * internal strings – do not free, DO copy if retained beyond processing.\n   346→ *\n   347→ * Return:\n   348→ *   1  – cache HIT  (payload populated)\n   349→ *   0  – cache MISS\n   350→ *  <0  – negative errno on internal failure\n   351→ */\n   352→int mm_cache_get(mm_cache_t *c,\n   353→                 const char *key,\n   354→                 unsigned char **payload_out,\n   355→                 size_t *payload_len_out,\n   356→                 const char **content_type_out,\n   357→                 const char **etag_out,\n   358→                 uint32_t *age_out)\n   359→{\n   360→    if (!c || !key || !payload_out || !payload_len_out)\n   361→        return -EINVAL;\n   362→\n   363→    pthread_rwlock_rdlock(&c->rwlock);\n   364→\n   365→    uint64_t hash = mm_fnv1a_hash(key, strlen(key));\n   366→    size_t idx = hash & (c->tbl_size - 1);\n   367→\n   368→    while (c->table[idx] && strcmp(c->table[idx]->key, key) != 0)\n   369→        idx = (idx + 1) & (c->tbl_size - 1);\n   370→\n   371→    mm_cache_entry_t *e = c->table[idx];\n   372→\n   373→    /* Fast path: MISS */\n   374→    if (!e) {\n   375→        c->misses++;\n   376→        pthread_rwlock_unlock(&c->rwlock);\n   377→        return 0;\n   378→    }\n   379→\n   380→    /* Check expiration */\n   381→    time_t now = time(NULL);\n   382→    if (e->expiry_ts < now) {\n   383→        /* Upgrade to write lock to remove expired entry */\n   384→        pthread_rwlock_unlock(&c->rwlock);\n   385→        pthread_rwlock_wrlock(&c->rwlock);\n   386→\n   387→        /* ensure another thread hasn't removed it */\n   388→        if (c->table[idx] == e) {\n   389→            /* Detach from LRU */\n   390→            if (e->prev) e->prev->next = e->next;\n   391→            if (e->next) e->next->prev = e->prev;\n   392→            if (e == c->lru_head) c->lru_head = e->next;\n   393→            if (e == c->lru_tail) c->lru_tail = e->prev;\n   394→\n   395→            /* Hash slot removal */\n   396→            c->table[idx] = NULL;\n   397→            c->entry_count--;\n   398→            c->current_bytes -= e->payload_len;\n   399→\n   400→            /* Rehash cluster (Robin Hood deletion not necessary for small table) */\n   401→            size_t j = (idx + 1) & (c->tbl_size - 1);\n   402→            while (c->table[j]) {\n   403→                mm_cache_entry_t *move = c->table[j];\n   404→                c->table[j] = NULL;\n   405→\n   406→                size_t new_idx = mm_fnv1a_hash(move->key, strlen(move->key)) & (c->tbl_size - 1);\n   407→                while (c->table[new_idx])\n   408→                    new_idx = (new_idx + 1) & (c->tbl_size - 1);\n   409→                c->table[new_idx] = move;\n   410→                j = (j + 1) & (c->tbl_size - 1);\n   411→            }\n   412→\n   413→            free(e->key);\n   414→            free(e->payload);\n   415→            free(e->content_type);\n   416→            free(e->etag);\n   417→            free(e);\n   418→        }\n   419→\n   420→        c->misses++;\n   421→        pthread_rwlock_unlock(&c->rwlock);\n   422→        return 0;\n   423→    }\n   424→\n   425→    /* HIT */\n   426→    e->last_access = (uint64_t)clock();\n   427→    c->hits++;\n   428→\n   429→    /* Copy payload for caller */\n   430→    unsigned char *payload_dup = malloc(e->payload_len);\n   431→    if (!payload_dup) {\n   432→        pthread_rwlock_unlock(&c->rwlock);\n   433→        return -ENOMEM;\n   434→    }\n   435→    memcpy(payload_dup, e->payload, e->payload_len);\n   436→\n   437→    *payload_out      = payload_dup;\n   438→    *payload_len_out  = e->payload_len;\n   439→    if (content_type_out) *content_type_out = e->content_type;\n   440→    if (etag_out)        *etag_out          = e->etag;\n   441→    if (age_out)         *age_out           = (uint32_t)(now - (e->expiry_ts - now));\n   442→\n   443→    /* Defer LRU touch after releasing read lock to cut contention.          */\n   444→    pthread_rwlock_unlock(&c->rwlock);\n   445→\n   446→    pthread_rwlock_wrlock(&c->rwlock);\n   447→    mm_cache_lru_touch(c, e);\n   448→    pthread_rwlock_unlock(&c->rwlock);\n   449→\n   450→    return 1;\n   451→}\n   452→\n   453→/*\n   454→ * mm_cache_invalidate\n   455→ * ----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   456→ * Removes a single entry from the cache (e.g. after POST/PUT/DELETE).\n   457→ */\n   458→void mm_cache_invalidate(mm_cache_t *c, const char *key)\n   459→{\n   460→    if (!c || !key) return;\n   461→    pthread_rwlock_wrlock(&c->rwlock);\n   462→\n   463→    uint64_t hash = mm_fnv1a_hash(key, strlen(key));\n   464→    size_t idx = hash & (c->tbl_size - 1);\n   465→\n   466→    while (c->table[idx] && strcmp(c->table[idx]->key, key) != 0)\n   467→        idx = (idx + 1) & (c->tbl_size - 1);\n   468→\n   469→    mm_cache_entry_t *e = c->table[idx];\n   470→    if (!e) {\n   471→        pthread_rwlock_unlock(&c->rwlock);\n   472→        return;\n   473→    }\n   474→\n   475→    /* Detach */\n   476→    if (e->prev) e->prev->next = e->next;\n   477→    if (e->next) e->next->prev = e->prev;\n   478→    if (e == c->lru_head) c->lru_head = e->next;\n   479→    if (e == c->lru_tail) c->lru_tail = e->prev;\n   480→\n   481→    c->table[idx] = NULL;\n   482→    c->entry_count--;\n   483→    c->current_bytes -= e->payload_len;\n   484→\n   485→    /* Rehash cluster */\n   486→    size_t j = (idx + 1) & (c->tbl_size - 1);\n   487→    while (c->table[j]) {\n   488→        mm_cache_entry_t *move = c->table[j];\n   489→        c->table[j] = NULL;\n   490→\n   491→        size_t new_idx = mm_fnv1a_hash(move->key, strlen(move->key)) & (c->tbl_size - 1);\n   492→        while (c->table[new_idx])\n   493→            new_idx = (new_idx + 1) & (c->tbl_size - 1);\n   494→        c->table[new_idx] = move;\n   495→\n   496→        j = (j + 1) & (c->tbl_size - 1);\n   497→    }\n   498→\n   499→    free(e->key);\n   500→    free(e->payload);\n   501→    free(e->content_type);\n   502→    free(e->etag);\n   503→    free(e);\n   504→\n   505→    pthread_rwlock_unlock(&c->rwlock);\n   506→    mm_log(LOG_DEBUG, \"Cache invalidated: %s\", key);\n   507→}\n   508→\n   509→/*\n   510→ * mm_cache_purge\n   511→ * ----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   512→ * Removes every entry from the cache.\n   513→ */\n   514→void mm_cache_purge(mm_cache_t *c)\n   515→{\n   516→    if (!c) return;\n   517→    pthread_rwlock_wrlock(&c->rwlock);\n   518→\n   519→    for (size_t i = 0; i < c->tbl_size; ++i) {\n   520→        mm_cache_entry_t *e = c->table[i];\n   521→        if (e) {\n   522→            free(e->key);\n   523→            free(e->payload);\n   524→            free(e->content_type);\n   525→            free(e->etag);\n   526→            free(e);\n   527→        }\n   528→        c->table[i] = NULL;\n   529→    }\n   530→\n   531→    c->entry_count   = 0;\n   532→    c->current_bytes = 0;\n   533→    c->lru_head = c->lru_tail = NULL;\n   534→\n   535→    pthread_rwlock_unlock(&c->rwlock);\n   536→    mm_log(LOG_INFO, \"Cache fully purged\");\n   537→}\n   538→\n   539→/*\n   540→ * mm_cache_metrics_snapshot\n   541→ * ----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   542→ * Captures a read-only snapshot of metrics for Prometheus scrape thread.\n   543→ */\n   544→void mm_cache_metrics_snapshot(const mm_cache_t *c,\n   545→                               mm_cache_metrics_t *out)\n   546→{\n   547→    if (!c || !out) return;\n   548→    pthread_rwlock_rdlock((pthread_rwlock_t *)&c->rwlock);\n   549→\n   550→    out->hits          = c->hits;\n   551→    out->misses        = c->misses;\n   552→    out->evictions     = c->evictions;\n   553→    out->entry_count   = c->entry_count;\n   554→    out->current_bytes = c->current_bytes;\n   555→\n   556→    pthread_rwlock_unlock((pthread_rwlock_t *)&c->rwlock);\n   557→}\n   558→\n   559→/* ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   560→ * INTERNAL HELPERS\n   561→ * -------------------------------------------------------------------------*/\n   562→\n   563→/* Fowler-Noll-Vo hash */\n   564→static uint64_t mm_fnv1a_hash(const void *data, size_t len)\n   565→{\n   566→    const uint8_t *p = (const uint8_t *)data;\n   567→    uint64_t h = 14695981039346656037ULL; /* offset basis */\n   568→    for (size_t i = 0; i < len; ++i) {\n   569→        h ^= (uint64_t)p[i];\n   570→        h *= 1099511628211ULL;            /* FNV prime    */\n   571→    }\n   572→    return h;\n   573→}\n   574→\n   575→/* Resize hash table (called under write lock) */\n   576→static int mm_cache_grow_table(mm_cache_t *c, size_t new_size)\n   577→{\n   578→    mm_cache_entry_t **new_tbl = calloc(new_size, sizeof(*new_tbl));\n   579→    if (!new_tbl)\n   580→        return ENOMEM;\n   581→\n   582→    if (c->table) {\n   583→        for (size_t i = 0; i < c->tbl_size; ++i) {\n   584→            mm_cache_entry_t *e = c->table[i];\n   585→            if (!e) continue;\n   586→\n   587→            size_t idx = mm_fnv1a_hash(e->key, strlen(e->key)) & (new_size - 1);\n   588→            while (new_tbl[idx])\n   589→                idx = (idx + 1) & (new_size - 1);\n   590→            new_tbl[idx] = e;\n   591→        }\n   592→        free(c->table);\n   593→    }\n   594→    c->table    = new_tbl;\n   595→    c->tbl_size = new_size;\n   596→    return 0;\n   597→}\n   598→\n   599→/* Move entry to head (MRU) */\n   600→static void mm_cache_lru_touch(mm_cache_t *c, mm_cache_entry_t *e)\n   601→{\n   602→    if (e == c->lru_head) return; /* already MRU */\n   603→\n   604→    /* detach */\n   605→    if (e->prev) e->prev->next = e->next;\n   606→    if (e->next) e->next->prev = e->prev;\n   607→    if (e == c->lru_tail) c->lru_tail = e->prev;\n   608→\n   609→    /* insert at head */\n   610→    e->prev = NULL;\n   611→    e->next = c->lru_head;\n   612→    if (c->lru_head) c->lru_head->prev = e;\n   613→    c->lru_head = e;\n   614→    if (!c->lru_tail) c->lru_tail = e;\n   615→}\n   616→\n   617→/* Evict until under capacity constraints */\n   618→static void mm_cache_evict_if_needed(mm_cache_t *c, size_t add_bytes)\n   619→{\n   620→    while ( (c->entry_count >= c->max_entries) ||\n   621→            (c->current_bytes + add_bytes > c->capacity_bytes) )\n   622→    {\n   623→        mm_cache_entry_t *victim = c->lru_tail;\n   624→        if (!victim) break; /* Shouldn't happen */\n   625→\n   626→        /* Detach from list */\n   627→        if (victim->prev) victim->prev->next = NULL;\n   628→        c->lru_tail = victim->prev;\n   629→        if (victim == c->lru_head)\n   630→            c->lru_head = NULL;\n   631→\n   632→        /* Remove from hash table */\n   633→        size_t idx = mm_fnv1a_hash(victim->key, strlen(victim->key)) & (c->tbl_size - 1);\n   634→        while (c->table[idx] && c->table[idx] != victim)\n   635→            idx = (idx + 1) & (c->tbl_size - 1);\n   636→        if (c->table[idx] == victim)\n   637→            c->table[idx] = NULL;\n   638→\n   639→        /* Rehash cluster to fill holes */\n   640→        size_t j = (idx + 1) & (c->tbl_size - 1);\n   641→        while (c->table[j]) {\n   642→            mm_cache_entry_t *move = c->table[j];\n   643→            c->table[j] = NULL;\n   644→\n   645→            size_t new_idx = mm_fnv1a_hash(move->key, strlen(move->key)) & (c->tbl_size - 1);\n   646→            while (c->table[new_idx])\n   647→                new_idx = (new_idx + 1) & (c->tbl_size - 1);\n   648→            c->table[new_idx] = move;\n   649→\n   650→            j = (j + 1) & (c->tbl_size - 1);\n         \"capacity=%zu KiB)\",\n                     MM_CACHE_MODULE_VERSION,\n                     max_entries,\n                     capacity_bytes >> 10);\n    return 0;\n}\n\n/*\n * mm_cache_destroy\n * ----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n * Frees all memory associated with the cache.\n */\nvoid mm_cache_destroy(mm_cache_t *c)\n{\n    if        return EINVAL;\n\n    if (strlen(key) > MM_CACHE_MAX_KEYLEN)\n        return ENAMETOOLONG;\n\n    if (ttl_seconds < MM_CACHE_MIN_TTL)\n        ttl_seconds = MM_CACHE_MIN_TTL;\n\n    /* existing->content_type  = new_entry->content_type;\n\n        free(existing->etag);\n        existing->etag          = new_entry->etag;\n\n        /* Touch */\n        mm_cache_lru_touch(c, Return:\n *   1  – cache HIT  (payload populated)\n *   0  – cache MISS\n *  <0  – negative errno on internal failure\n */\nint mm_cache_get(mm_cache_t *c,\n                 const char *key,\n                 unsigned char **payload_out,\n                 size_t *payload_len_out,\n                 const char **content_type_out,\n                 const char **etag_out,\n                 uint32_t *age_out)\n{\n    if (!c || !key || !payload_out || !payload_len_out)\n        return -EINVAL;\n\n    pthread_rwlock_rdlock(&c->rwlock);\n\n    uint64_t hash = mm_fnv1a_hash(key, strlen(key));\n    size_t idx = hash & (c->tbl_size - 1);\n\n    while (c->table[idx] && strcmp(c->table[idx]->key, key) != 0)\n        idx = (idx + 1) & (c->tbl_size - 1);\n\n    mm_cache_entry_t *e = c->table[idx];\n\n    /* Fast path: MISS */\n    if (!e) {\n        c->misses++;\n        pthread_rwlock_unlock(&c->rwlock);\n        return 0;\n    }\n\n    /* Check expiration */\n    time_t now = time(NULL);\n    if (e->expiry_ts < now) {\n        /* Upgrade to write lock to remove expired entry */\n        pthread_rwlock_unlock(&c->rwlock);\n        pthread_rwlock_wrlock(&c->rwlock);\n\n        /* ensure another thread hasn't removed it */\n        if (c->table[idx] == e) {\n            /* Detach from LRU */\n            if (e->prev) e->prev->next = e->next;\n            if (e->next) e->next->prev = e->prev;\n            if (e == c->lru_head) c->lru_head = e->next;\n            if (e == c->lru_tail) c->lru_tail = e->prev;\n\n            /* Hash slot removal */\n            c->table[idx] = NULL;\n            c->entry_count--;\n            c->current_bytes -= e->payload_len;\n\n            /* Rehash cluster (Robin Hood deletion not necessary for small table) */\n new_idx = mm_fnv1a_hash(move->key, strlen(move->key)) & (c->tbl_size - 1);\n                while (c->table[new_idx])\n                    new_idx = (new_idx + 1) & (c->tbl_size - 1);\n                c->table[new_idx] = move;\n                j = (j + 1) & (c->tbl_size - 1);\n            }\n\n            free(e->key);\n            free(e->payload);\n            free(e->content_type);\n            free(e->etag);\n            free(e);\n        }\n\n        c->misses++;\n        pthread_rwlock_unlock(&c->rwlock);\n        return 0;\n    }\n\n    /* HIT */\n    e->last_access = (uint64_t)clock();\n    c->hits++;\n\n    /* Copy payload for caller */\n    unsigned char *payload_dup = malloc(e->payload_len);\n    if (!payload_dup) {\n        pthread_rwlock_unlock(&c->rwlock);\n        return -ENOMEM;\n    }\n    memcpy(payload_dup, e->payload, e->payload_len);\n\n    *payload_out      = payload_dup;\n    *payload_len_out  = e->payload_len;\n    if (content_type_out) touch after releasing read lock to cut contention.          */\n    pthread_rwlock_unlock(&c->rwlock);\n\n    pthread_rwlock_wrlock(&c->rwlock);\n    mm_cache_lru_touch(c, e);\n    pthread_rwlock_unlock(&c->rwlock);\n\n    return 1;\n}\n\n/*\n * mm_cache_invalidate\n * ----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n * Removes a single entry from the cache (e.g. after POST/PUT/DELETE).\n */\nvoid mm_cache_invalidate(mm_cache_t *c, const char *key)\n{\n    if (!c || !key) return;\n    pthread_rwlock_wrlock(&c->rwlock);\n\n    uint64_t hash = mm_fnv1a_hash(key, strlen(key));\n    size_t idx = hash & (c->tbl_size - 1);\n\n    while (c->table[idx] && strcmp(c->table[idx]->key, key) != 0)\n        idx = (idx + 1) & (c->tbl_size - 1);\n\n    mm_cache_entry_t *e = c->table[idx];\n    if (!e) {\n        pthread_rwlock_unlock(&c->rwlock);\n        return;\n    }\n\n    /* Detach */\n    if (e->prev) e->prev->next = e->next;\n    if (e->next) -= e->payload_len;\n\n    /* Rehash cluster */\n    size_t j = (idx + 1) & (c->tbl_size - 1);\n    while (c->table[j]) {\n        mm_cache_entry_t *move = c->table[j];\n        c->table[j] = NULL;\n\n c->table[new_idx] = move;\n\n        j = (j + 1) & (c->tbl_size - 1);\n    }\n\n    free(e->key);\n    free(e->payload);\n    free(e->content_type);\n    free(e->etag);\n    free(e);\n\n    pthread_rwlock_unlock(&c->rwlock);\n    mm_log(LOG_DEBUG, \"Cache invalidated: %s\", key);\n}\n\n/*\n * mm_cache_purge\n * ----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n * Removes every entry from the cache.\n */\nvoid mm_cache_purge(mm_cache_t *c)\n{\n    if (!c) return;\n    pthread_rwlock_wrlock(&c->rwlock);\n\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < c->tbl_size; ++i) {\n        mm_cache_entry_t *e = c->table[i];\n        if (e) {\n            free(e->key);\n            free(e->payload);\n            free(e->content_type);\n            free(e->etag);\n            free(e);\n        }\n        c->table[i] = NULL;\n    }\n\n    c->entry_count   = 0;\n    c->current_bytes = 0;\n    c->lru_head = c->lru_tail = NULL;\n\n    pthread_rwlock_unlock(&c->rwlock);\n    mm_log(LOG_INFO, \"Cache fully purged\");\n}\n\n/*\n * mm_cache_metrics_snapshot\n * ----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n * Captures a read-only snapshot of metrics for Prometheus scrape thread.\n */\nvoid mm_cache_metrics_snapshot(const mm_cache_t *c,\n                               mm_cache_metrics_t *out)\n{\n    if (!c || !out) return;\n    pthread_rwlock_rdlock((pthread_rwlock_t *)&c->rwlock);\n\n    out->hits          = c->hits;\n    out->misses        = c->misses;\n    out->evictions     = c->evictions;\n    out->entry_count   = c->entry_count;\n    out->current_bytes = c->current_bytes;\n\n    pthread_rwlock_unlock((pthread_rwlock_t *)&c->rwlock);\n}\n\n/* ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n * INTERNAL HELPERS\n * -------------------------------------------------------------------------*/\n\n/* Fowler-Noll-Vo hash */\nstatic uint64_t mm_fnv1a_hash(const void *data, size_t len)\n{\n    const uint8_t *p = (const uint8_t *)data;\n    uint64_t h = 14695981039346656037ULL; /* offset basis */\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < len; ++i) {\n        h ^= (uint64_t)p[i];\n        h *= 1099511628211ULL;            /* FNV prime    */\n    }\n    return h;\n}\n\n/* Resize hash table (called under write lock) */\nstatic int mm_cache_grow_table(mm_cache_t *c, size_t new_size)\n{\n    mm_cache_entry_t **new_tbl = calloc(new_size, sizeof(*new_tbl));\n    if (!new_tbl)\n        return ENOMEM;\n\n    if (c->table) {\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < c->tbl_size; ++i) {\n            mm_cache_entry_t *e = (new_size - 1);\n            new_tbl[idx] = e;\n        }\n        free(c->table);\n    }\n    c->table    = new_tbl;\n    c->tbl_size = new_size;\n    return 0;\n}\n\n/* Move entry to head (MRU) */\nstatic void mm_cache_lru_touch(mm_cache_t *c, mm_cache_entry_t *e)\n{\n    if (e == c->lru_head) return; /* already MRU */\n\n    /* detach */\n    if (e->prev) e->prev->next = e->next;\n    if (e->next) e->next->prev = e->prev;\n    if (e == c->lru_tail) c->lru_tail = e->prev;\n\n    /* insert at head */\n    e->prev = NULL;\n    e->next = c->lru_head;\n    if (c->lru_head) c->lru_head->prev = e;\n    c->lru_head = e;\n    if (!c->lru_tail) c->lru_tail = e;\n}\n\n/* Evict until under capacity constraints */\nstatic void mm_cache_evict_if_needed(mm_cache_t *c, size_t add_bytes)\n{\n    while ( (c->entry_count >= c->max_entries) ||\n            (c->current_bytes + add_bytes > c->capacity_bytes) )\n    {\n        mm_cache_entry_t *victim = c->lru_tail;\n        if (!victim) break; /* Shouldn't happen */\n\n        /* Detach from list */\n        if (victim->prev) victim->prev->next = NULL;\n        c->lru_tail = victim->prev;\n        if (victim == c->lru_head)\n            c->lru_head = NULL;\n\n        /* Remove from hash table */\n        size_t idx = mm_fnv1a_hash(victim->key, strlen(victim->key)) & (c->tbl_size - 1);\n        while (c->table[idx] && c->table[idx] != victim)\n            idx = (idx + 1) & (c->tbl_size - 1);\n        if (c->table[idx] == victim)\n            c->table[idx] = NULL;\n\n        /* Rehash cluster to fill holes */\n        size_t j = (idx + 1) & (c->tbl_size - 1);\n        while (c->table[j]) {\n            mm_cache_entry_t *move = c->table[j];\n            c->table[j] = NULL;\n\n            size_t new_idx = mm_fnv1a_hash(move->key, strlen(move->key)) & (c->tbl_size - 1);\n            while (c->table[new_idx])\n                new_idx = (new_idx + 1) & (c->tbl_size - 1);\n            c->table[new_idx] = move;\n\n            j = (j + 1) & (c->tbl_size - 1);\n        }\n\n        c->entry_count--;\n        c->current_bytes -= victim->payload_len;\n        c->evictions++;\n\n        mm_log(LOG_DEBUG, \"Cache evicted key=%s (payload=%zuB)\", victim->key, victim->payload_len);\n\n        free(victim->key);\n        free(victim->payload);\n        free(victim->content_type);\n        free(victim->etag);\n        free(victim);\n\n        if (c->entry_count == 0)\n            break;\n    }\n}\n\n/* ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n * UNIT 320→int mm_cache_invalidate(const char *uri_prefix)\n   321→{\n   322→    if (!uri_prefix) { return -EINVAL; }\n   323→\n   324→    pthread_mutex_lock(&g_ctx.mtx);\n   325→    cache_entry_t *entry, 340→/*---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   341→ *  Internal: Evict LRU until we have room for bytes_needed or within limit\n   342→ *---------------------------------------------------------------------------*/\n   343→static void mm_cache_evict_until_fit(size_t bytes_needed)\n   344→{\n   345→    time_t now = time(NULL);\n   346→    while ((g_ctx.bytes_in_use + bytes_needed) > g_ctx.max_bytes &&\n   347→            g_ctx.lru_tail != NULL) {\n   348→        cache_entry_t *victim = g_ctx.lru_tail;\n   349→\n   350→        /* LOG_TRACE(\"response_cache: evict LRU '%s' (size=%zu, age=%lds)\",\n   356→                  victim->key, victim->size, (long)(now - victim->expires_at));\n   357→\n   358→        mm_cache_dispose_entry(victim);\n   359→        mm_metrics_inc(\"response_cache_evictions_total\");\n   360→    }\n   361→\n   362→    /* Also drop stale items regardless of size */\n   363→    cache_entry_t *entry, *tmp;\n   364→    HASH_ITER(hh, g_ctx.table, entry, tmp) {\n   365→        if (entry->expires_at < now) {\n   366→            mm_cache_unlink(entry);\n   367→            HASH_DEL(g_ctx.table, entry);\n   368→            g_ctx.bytes_in_use -= entry->size;\n   369→            mm_cache_dispose_entry(entry);\n   370→            mm_metrics_inc(\"response_cache_evictions_total\");\n   371→        }\n   372→    }\n   373→}\n   374→\n   375→/*---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   376→ *  Internal: Insert entry at MRU head\n   377→ *---------------------------------------------------------------------------*/\n   378→static void mm_cache_link_mru(cache_entry_t *entry)\n   379→{\n   380→    entry->prev = NULL;\n   381→    entry->next = g_ctx.lru_head;\n   382→    if (g_ctx.lru_head) {\n   383→        g_ctx.lru_head->prev = entry;\n   384→    }\n   385→    g_ctx.lru_head = entry;\n   386→    if (!g_ctx.lru_tail) {\n   387→        g_ctx.lru_tail = entry;\n   388→    }\n   389→}\n   390→\n   391→/*---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   392→ *  Internal: Remove entry from LRU list\n   393→ *---------------------------------------------------------------------------*/\n   394→static void mm_cache_unlink(cache_entry_t *entry)\n   395→{\n   396→    if (entry->prev) {\n   397→        entry->prev->next = entry->next;\n   398→    } else {\n   399→        g_ctx.lru_head = entry->next;\n   400→    }\n   401→\n   402→    if (entry->next) {\n   403→        entry->next->prev = Designed to\n *   accelerate idempotent REST/GraphQL calls by short-circuiting the service\n *   pipeline when a fresh representation is already available.  Integrated\n *   with the internal event-bus so that writes to mutable resources trigger\n *   automatic cache-invalidation, guaranteeing strong consistency semantics\n *   while preserving performance for read-heavy traffic.\n *\n *   Highlights:\n *     • Lock-striped (single global mutex is OK for <50k QPS, upgrade later).\n *     • TTL-aware entries + proactive stale sweep on write contention.\n *     • Uses uthash (https://troydhanson.github.io/uthash/) for O(1) lookups.\n *     • Portable: C11, POSIX.1-2008.\n *\n * Copyright:\n *   © 2024 MercuryMonolith Contributors.  All rights reserved.\n */\n\n#include <ctype.h>\n#include <errno.h>\n#include <pthread.h>\n#include <stdarg.h>\n#include <stdbool.h>\n#include <stdint.h>\n#include <stdio.h>\n#include <stdlib.h>\n#include <string.h>\n#include <time.h>\n\n#include \"uthash.h\"            /* Header-only hash-table                     */\n#include \"mm_eventbus.h\"       /* Event bus API (publish/subscribe)          */\n#include \"mm_logger.h\"         /* Structured logging                         */\n#include \"mm_metrics.h\"        /* Prometheus counter helpers                 */\n#include \"mm_response_cache.h\" /* Public header for this implementation      */\n\n/*---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n *  Compile-time Tunables\n *---------------------------------------------------------------------------*/\n#ifndef MM_CACHE_DEFAULT_TTL_SEC\n#   define MM_CACHE_DEFAULT_TTL_SEC  30U    /* 30 seconds default TTL       */\n#endif\n\n#ifndef MM_CACHE_MAX_SIZE_BYTES\n#   define MM_CACHE_MAX_SIZE_BYTES   (128 * 1024 * 1024ULL) /* 128 MiB      */\n#endif\n\n#ifndef MM_CACHE_SWEEP_THRESHOLD\n#   define MM_CACHE_SWEEP_THRESHOLD  64     /* Sweep stale entries every N  */\n#endif\n\n/*---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n *  Data Structures\n *---------------------------------------------------------------------------*/\ntypedef struct cache_entry_t {\n    /* ---- Hash Key ---- */\n    char             *key;          /* Normalized cache key                  */\n    UT_hash_handle    hh;           /* uthash handle                         */\n\n    /* ---- Payload ----  */\n    uint8_t          *blob;         /* Serialized HTTP/GraphQL response      */\n    size_t            size;         /* Payload length                        */\n    time_t            expires_at;   /* Absolute unix epoch                   */\n\n    /* ---- LRU List ---- */\n    struct cache_entry_t *prev;\n    struct cache_entry_t *next;\n} cache_entry_t;\n\ntypedef struct mm_cache_ctx_t {\n    cache_entry_t *table;           /* Hash table root                       */\n    cache_entry_t *lru_head;        /* Most recently used                    */\n    cache_entry_t *lru_tail;        /* Least recently used                   */\n\n    size_t          bytes_in_use;   /* Total memory footprint                */\n    size_t          max_bytes;      /* Evict when > max_bytes                */\n\n    pthread_mutex_t mtx;            /* Global lock                           */\n\n    uint64_t        op_counter;     /* Incremented each GET/PUT → sweep      */\n} mm_cache_ctx_t;\n\n/* Singleton instance (init during mm_cache_init) */\nstatic mm_cache_ctx_t g_ctx = {\n    .table        = NULL,\n    .lru_head     = NULL,\n    .lru_tail     = NULL,\n    .bytes_in_use = 0,\n    .max_bytes    = MM_CACHE_MAX_SIZE_BYTES,\n    .mtx          = PTHREAD_MUTEX_INITIALIZER,\n    .op_counter   = 0\n};\n\n/*---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n *  Forward Declarations\n *---------------------------------------------------------------------------*/\nstatic void  mm_cache_evict_until_fit(size_t bytes_needed);\nstatic void  mm_cache_link_mru(cache_entry_t *entry);\nstatic void  mm_cache_unlink(cache_entry_t *entry);\nstatic void  mm_cache_dispose_entry(cache_entry_t *entry);\nstatic void* mm_cache_event_listener(const mm_event_t *ev, void *udata);\n\n/*---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n *  Helper: Normalize cache key\n *\n * The cache key is constructed as:\n *   <METHOD> ':' <URI_PATH> '?' <SORTED_QUERY> '|' <ACCEPT>\n *\n * This avoids duplicates due to param ordering or case differences.\n *---------------------------------------------------------------------------*/\nstatic char *build_cache_key(const char *method,\n                             const char *uri,\n                             const char *accept)\n{\n    if (!method || !uri || !accept) { return NULL; }\n\n    /* -- Case-fold HTTP method (GET, HEAD, etc.) -- */\n    char method_up[16] = {0};\n    size_t i = 0;\n    for (; i < sizeof method_up - 1 && method[i]; ++i) {\n        method_up[i] = (char)toupper((unsigned char)method[i]);\n    }\n    method_up[i] = '\\0';\n\n    /* TODO: canonicalize query-string ordering (requires parser) */\n    /* For simplicity in this module, we assume caller provides sorted URI. */\n\n    size_t key_len = strlen(method_up) + 1        /* ':' */\n                   + strlen(uri) + 1              /* '|' */\n                   + strlen(accept) + 1;          /* NUL */\n\n    char *key = malloc(key_len);\n    if (!key) { return NULL; }\n\n    snprintf(key, key_len, \"%s:%s|%s\", method_up, uri, accept);\n    return key;\n}\n\n/*---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n *  API: mm_cache_init\n *---------------------------------------------------------------------------*/\nint mm_cache_init(size_t max_size_bytes)\n{\n    pthread_mutex_lock(&g_ctx.mtx);\n    if (max_size_bytes > 0) {\n        g_ctx.max_bytes = max_size_bytes;\n    }\n    /* Subscribe to event-bus for invalidation notifications */\n    if (mm_eventbus_subscribe(\"resource.updated\", mm_cache_event_listener, NULL) != 0 ||\n        mm_eventbus_subscribe(\"resource.deleted\", mm_cache_event_listener, NULL) != 0) {\n        pthread_mutex_unlock(&g_ctx.mtx);\n        return -1;\n    }\n\n    pthread_mutex_unlock(&g_ctx.mtx);\n    LOG_INFO(\"response_cache: initialized (capacity=%zu bytes)\", g_ctx.max_bytes);\n    return 0;\n}\n\n/*---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n *  API: mm_cache_shutdown\n *---------------------------------------------------------------------------*/\nvoid mm_cache_shutdown(void)\n{\n    pthread_mutex_lock(&g_ctx.mtx);\n\n    cache_entry_t *entry, *tmp;\n    HASH_ITER(hh, g_ctx.table, entry, tmp) {\n        HASH_DEL(g_ctx.table, entry);\n        mm_cache_dispose_entry(entry);\n    }\n    g_ctx.lru_head = g_ctx.lru_tail = NULL;\n    g_ctx.bytes_in_use = 0;\n\n    pthread_mutex_unlock(&g_ctx.mtx);\n\n    mm_eventbus_unsubscribe(\"resource.updated\", mm_cache_event_listener);\n    mm_eventbus_unsubscribe(\"resource.deleted\", mm_cache_event_listener);\n}\n\n/*---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n *  API: mm_cache_get\n *---------------------------------------------------------------------------*/\nint mm_cache_get(const char *method,\n                 const char *uri,\n                 const char *accept,\n                 uint8_t   **out_blob,\n                 size_t     *out_size)\n{\n    if (!out_blob || !out_size) { return -EINVAL; }\n    *out_blob = NULL;\n    *out_size = 0;\n\n    char *key = build_cache_key(method, uri, accept);\n    if (!key) { return -ENOMEM; }\n\n    int rc = -ENOENT;\n\n    pthread_mutex_lock(&g_ctx.mtx);\n    cache_entry_t *entry = NULL;\n    HASH_FIND_STR(g_ctx.table, key, entry);\n    if (entry) {\n        if (entry->expires_at < time(NULL)) {\n            /* Stale — evict */\n            LOG_DEBUG(\"response_cache: stale hit '%s' (removing)\", key);\n            mm_cache_unlink(entry);\n            HASH_DEL(g_ctx.table, entry);\n            g_ctx.bytes_in_use -= entry->size;\n            mm_cache_dispose_entry(entry);\n            rc = -EAGAIN; /* treat as miss */\n        } else {\n            /* Cache hit */\n            *out_size = entry->size;\n            *out_blob = malloc(entry->size);\n            if (*out_blob) {\n                memcpy(*out_blob, entry->blob, entry->size);\n                mm_metrics_inc(\"response_cache_hits_total\");\n                rc = 0;\n            } else {\n                rc = -ENOMEM;\n            }\n\n            /* Move entry to MRU position */\n            mm_cache_unlink(entry);\n            mm_cache_link_mru(entry);\n        }\n    } else {\n        mm_metrics_inc(\"response_cache_misses_total\");\n    }\n\n    g_ctx.op_counter++;\n    if (g_ctx.op_counter % MM_CACHE_SWEEP_THRESHOLD == 0) {\n        mm_cache_evict_until_fit(0); /* Sweep stale entries opportunistically */\n    }\n\n    pthread_mutex_unlock(&g_ctx.mtx);\n    free(key);\n    return rc;\n}\n\n/*---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n *  API: mm_cache_put\n *---------------------------------------------------------------------------*/\nint mm_cache_put(const char *method,\n                 const char *uri,\n                 const char *accept,\n                 const uint8_t *blob,\n                 size_t blob_size,\n                 unsigned ttl_sec)\n{\n    if (!method || !uri || !accept || !blob || blob_size == 0) {\n        return -EINVAL;\n    }\n\n    char *key = build_cache_key(method, uri, accept);\n    if (!key) { return -ENOMEM; }\n\n    pthread_mutex_lock(&g_ctx.mtx);\n\n    /* If entry exists, overwrite */\n    cache_entry_t *entry = NULL;\n    HASH_FIND_STR(g_ctx.table, key, entry);\n    if (entry) {\n        mm_cache_unlink(entry);\n        g_ctx.bytes_in_use -= entry->size;\n        free(entry->blob);\n        entry->blob = NULL;\n    } else {\n        entry = calloc(1, sizeof *entry);\n        if (!entry) {\n            pthread_mutex_unlock(&g_ctx.mtx);\n            free(key);\n            return -ENOMEM;\n        }\n        entry->key = key; /* Take ownership */\n    }\n\n    /* Copy payload */\n    entry->blob = malloc(blob_size);\n    if (!entry->blob) {\n        pthread_mutex_unlock(&g_ctx.mtx);\n        free(entry->key);\n        free(entry);\n        free(key);\n        return -ENOMEM;\n    }\n    memcpy(entry->blob, blob, blob_size);\n    entry->size        = blob_size;\n    entry->expires_at  = time(NULL) + (ttl_sec ? ttl_sec : MM_CACHE_DEFAULT_TTL_SEC);\n\n    /* Insert/Update hash table */\n    HASH_REPLACE(hh, g_ctx.table, key, strlen(key), entry);\n\n    /* Insert into LRU at MRU position */\n    mm_cache_link_mru(entry);\n\n    g_ctx.bytes_in_use += blob_size;\n    mm_metrics_inc(\"response_cache_puts_total\");\n\n    /* Evict if needed */\n    mm_cache_evict_until_fit(0);\n\n    g_ctx.op_counter++;\n    pthread_mutex_unlock(&g_ctx.mtx);\n\n    if (entry != NULL) {\n        LOG_DEBUG(\"response_cache: stored '%s' (size=%zu, ttl=%u)\", key, blob_size, ttl_sec);\n    }\n    return 0;\n}\n\n/*---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n *  API: mm_cache_invalidate\n *\n *  Invalidate by exact resource URI (all methods & accept headers).\n *---------------------------------------------------------------------------*/\nint mm_cache_invalidate(const char *uri_prefix)\n{\n    if (!uri_prefix) { return -EINVAL; }\n\n    pthread_mutex_lock(&g_ctx.mtx);\n    cache_entry_t *entry, *tmp;\n    HASH_ITER(hh, g_ctx.table, entry, tmp) {\n        if (strstr(entry->key, uri_prefix)) {\n            LOG_DEBUG(\"response_cache: invalidating '%s' (prefix='%s')\",\n                      entry->key, uri_prefix);\n            mm_cache_unlink(entry);\n            HASH_DEL(g_ctx.table, entry);\n            g_ctx.bytes_in_use -= entry->size;\n            mm_cache_dispose_entry(entry);\n        }\n    }\n    pthread_mutex_unlock(&g_ctx.mtx);\n    return 0;\n}\n\n/*---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n *  Internal: Evict LRU until we have room for bytes_needed or within limit\n *---------------------------------------------------------------------------*/\nstatic void mm_cache_evict_until_fit(size_t bytes_needed)\n{\n    time_t now = time(NULL);\n    while ((g_ctx.bytes_in_use + bytes_needed) > g_ctx.max_bytes &&\n            g_ctx.lru_tail != NULL) {\n        cache_entry_t *victim = g_ctx.lru_tail;\n\n        /* Remove */\n        mm_cache_unlink(victim);\n        HASH_DEL(g_ctx.table, victim);\n        g_ctx.bytes_in_use -= victim->size;\n\n        LOG_TRACE(\"response_cache: evict LRU '%s' (size=%zu, age=%lds)\",\n                  victim->key, victim->size, (long)(now - victim->expires_at));\n\n        mm_cache_dispose_entry(victim);\n        mm_metrics_inc(\"response_cache_evictions_total\");\n    }\n\n    /* Also drop stale items regardless of size */\n    cache_entry_t *entry, *tmp;\n    HASH_ITER(hh, g_ctx.table, entry, tmp) {\n        if (entry->expires_at < now) {\n            mm_cache_unlink(entry);\n            HASH_DEL(g_ctx.table, entry);\n            g_ctx.bytes_in_use -= entry->size;\n            mm_cache_dispose_entry(entry);\n            mm_metrics_inc(\"response_cache_evictions_total\");\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n/*---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n *  Internal: Insert entry at MRU head\n *---------------------------------------------------------------------------*/\nstatic void mm_cache_link_mru(cache_entry_t *entry)\n{\n    entry->prev = NULL;\n    entry->next = g_ctx.lru_head;\n    if (g_ctx.lru_head) {\n        g_ctx.lru_head->prev = entry;\n    }\n    g_ctx.lru_head = entry;\n    if (!g_ctx.lru_tail) {\n        g_ctx.lru_tail = entry;\n    }\n}\n\n/*---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n *  Internal: Remove entry from LRU list\n *---------------------------------------------------------------------------*/\nstatic void mm_cache_unlink(cache_entry_t *entry)\n{\n    if (entry->prev) {\n        entry->prev->next = entry->next;\n    } else {\n        g_ctx.lru_head = entry->next;\n    }\n\n    if (entry->next) {\n        entry->next->prev = entry->prev;\n    } else {\n        g_ctx.lru_tail = entry->prev;\n    }\n\n    entry->prev = entry->next = NULL;\n}\n\n/*---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n *  Internal: Destroy entry and its resources\n *---------------------------------------------------------------------------*/\nstatic void mm_cache_dispose_entry(cache_entry_t *entry)\n{\n    if (!entry) return;\n    free(entry->key);\n    free(entry->blob);\n    free(entry);\n}\n\n/*---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n *  Internal: Event-bus listener → Invalidate on writes\n *---------------------------------------------------------------------------*/\nstatic void* mm_cache_event_listener(const mm_event_t *ev, void *udata)\n{\n    (void)udata;\n    if (!ev || !ev->topic || !ev->payload) { return NULL; }\n\n    const char *resource_uri = (const char *)ev->payload;\n    LOG_DEBUG(\"response_cache: received '%s' event (uri=%s)\",\n              ev->topic, resource_uri);\n\n    mm_cache_invalidate(resource_uri);\n    return NULL;\n}\n\n/*---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n *  Debug/Diagnostics: Dump cache stats (optional)\n *---------------------------------------------------------------------------*/\nvoid mm_cache_dump_stats(FILE *out)\n{\n    if (!out) { out = stderr; }\n\n    pthread_mutex_lock(&g_ctx.mtx);\n    fprintf(out,\n            \"=== Response Cache Stats ===\\n\"\n            \" entries:      %u\\n\"\n            \" bytes_in_use: %zu\\n\"\n            \" capacity:     %zu\\n\",\n            HASH_COUNT(g_ctx.table),\n            g_ctx.bytes_in_use,\n            g_ctx.max_bytes);\n    pthread_mutex_unlock(&g_ctx.mtx);\n}\n\n/*==========================================================================*/\n","numLines":458,"startLine":1,"totalLines":458}}}

(a->tv_sec == b->tv_sec && a->tv_nsec > b->tv_nsec);\n   203→}\n   204→\n   205→/* ts_out = ts_in + delta_ms */\n   206→static inline void\n   207→_add_ms(struct timespec *out, const struct timespec 217→/*----------------------------------------------------------------------\n   218→ * LRU list helpers  (internal, caller must hold write-lock!)\n   219→ *----------------------------------------------------------------------*/\n   220→static void\n   221→_lru_move_to_front(mmch_response_cache_t *cache, mmch_cache_entry_t *entry)\n   222→{\n   223→    if (cache->lru_head == entry) return; /* already at front */\n   224→\n   225→    /* detach */\n   226→    if (entry->prev) entry->prev->next = entry->next;\n   227→    if (entry->next) *----------------------------------------------------------------------*/\n   263→\n   264→/*\n   265→ * mmch_cache_create\n   266→ * -----------------\n   267→ * Allocate & initialise a new cache   272→{\n   273→    mmch_response_cache_t *cache = calloc(1, sizeof(*cache));\n   274→    if (!cache) return NULL;\n   275→\n   276→    cache->max_entries = (max_entries == 0) ? 1024 : max_entries;\n The payload is deep-copied.\n   311→ *\n   312→ * ttl_ms == 0  -> no expiry (discouraged, but supported).\n   313→ *\n   314→ * Returns:\n   315→ *   0  on success\n   316→ *  -ENOMEM  allocation failure\n   317→ *  -EINVAL  invalid args\n   318→ */\n   319→int\n   320→mmch_cache_put(mmch_response_cache_t *cache,\n   321→               const char *key,\n   322→               const void *payload,\n   323→               size_t payload_sz,\n   324→               uint32_t ttl_ms)\n   325→{\n   326→    if (!cache || !key || !payload || payload_sz == 0)\n   327→        return -EINVAL;\n   328→\n   329→    int rc = 0;\n   330→    mmch_cache_entry_t *entry = NULL;\n   331→\n   332→    /* pre-allocate outside lock to reduce contention */\n   333→    entry = calloc(1, sizeof(*entry));\n   334→    if (!entry) return -ENOMEM;\n   335→\n   336→    entry->key = strndup(key, strlen(key));\n   337→    if (!entry->key) { free(entry);                        \\\n    unsigned _hf_i,_hf_hashv=5381;                                                     \\\n    for(_hf_i=0; _hf_i< keylen; _hf_i++)                                               \\\n        _hf_hashv = ((_hf_hashv << 5) + _hf_hashv) ^ _hf_key[_hf_i];                   \\\n    hashv = _hf_hashv;                                                                 \\\n} while(0)\n\ntypedef struct UT_hash_handle {\n    struct UT_hash_table *tbl;\n    struct UT_hash_handle *prev;      /* previous element in app order      */\n    struct UT_hash_handle *next;      /* next element in app order          */\n    unsigned                hashv;    /* hash value                         */\n} UT_hash_handle;\n\n#endif /* UTHASH_H */\n/*  End of inlined uthash.h *********************************************/\n\n/*----------------------------------------------------------------------\n * Forward-declarations for project-wide facilities\n *----------------------------------------------------------------------*/\n#ifdef __cplusplus\nextern \"C\" {\n#endif\n\n/* Centralised, structured logger (json / text). Not included here */\nvoid mmch_log_error(const char *facility, const char *fmt, ...);\nvoid mmch_log_debug(const char *facility, const char *fmt, ...);\n\n/* Metrics abstraction (Prometheus). Not included here */\nvoid mmch_metrics_inc_counter(const char *name);\nvoid mmch_metrics_set_gauge(const char *name, double value);\n\n#ifdef __cplusplus\n}\n#endif\n\n/*----------------------------------------------------------------------\n * Module-local definitions\n *----------------------------------------------------------------------*/\n#define MODULE_FACILITY \"response_cache\"\n#define NSECS_IN_MSEC   1000000ULL\n\ntypedef struct mmch_cache_entry {\n    char               *key;           /* immutable request hash / route */\n    unsigned            key_len;\n\n    void               *payload;       /* heap-allocated opaque blob    */\n    size_t              payload_sz;\n\n    struct timespec     expiry;        /* monotonic deadline            */\n    struct timespec     last_access;   /* for LRU ordering              */\n\n    /* intrusive data structures */\n    UT_hash_handle      hh;            /* uthash handle (must be last?) */\n    struct mmch_cache_entry *prev;     /* prev in LRU doubly list       */\n    struct mmch_cache_entry *next;     /* next in LRU doubly list       */\n} mmch_cache_entry_t;\n\ntypedef struct mmch_response_cache {\n    mmch_cache_entry_t *table;         /* hash table root               */\n    mmch_cache_entry_t *lru_head;      /* most-recently used            */\n    mmch_cache_entry_t *lru_tail;      /* least-recently used           */\n\n    size_t              max_entries;   /* capacity                      */\n    size_t              curr_entries;  /* current load                  */\n\n    pthread_rwlock_t    rwlock;        /* multi-reader / single writer  */\n} mmch_response_cache_t;\n\n/*----------------------------------------------------------------------\n * Helper utilities\n *----------------------------------------------------------------------*/\n\n/* Return CLOCK_MONOTONIC time as struct timespec */\nstatic inline void\n_now(struct timespec *ts)\n{\n    clock_gettime(CLOCK_MONOTONIC, ts);\n}\n\n/* ts1 > ts2 ? */\nstatic inline int\n_ts_greater(const struct timespec *a, const struct timespec *b)\n{\n    return (a->tv_sec > b->tv_sec) ||\n           (a->tv_sec == b->tv_sec && a->tv_nsec > b->tv_nsec);\n}\n\n/* ts_out = ts_in + delta_ms */\nstatic inline void\n_add_ms(struct timespec *out, const struct timespec *in, uint32_t delta_ms)\n{\n    out->tv_sec  = in->tv_sec + delta_ms / 1000;\n    out->tv_nsec = in->tv_nsec + (delta_ms % 1000) * NSECS_IN_MSEC;\n    if (out->tv_nsec >= 1000000000L) {\n        out->tv_sec  += 1;\n        out->tv_nsec -= 1000000000L;\n    }\n}\n\n/*----------------------------------------------------------------------\n * LRU list helpers  (internal, caller must hold write-lock!)\n *----------------------------------------------------------------------*/\nstatic void\n_lru_move_to_front(mmch_response_cache_t *cache, mmch_cache_entry_t *entry)\n{\n    if (cache->lru_head == entry) return; /* already at front */\n\n    /* detach */\n    if (entry->prev) entry->prev->next = entry->next;\n    if (entry->next) entry->next->prev = entry->prev;\n    if (cache->lru_tail == entry) cache->lru_tail = entry->prev;\n\n    /* insert at head */\n    entry->prev = NULL;\n    entry->next = cache->lru_head;\n    if (cache->lru_head) cache->lru_head->prev = entry;\n    cache->lru_head = entry;\n    if (!cache->lru_tail) cache->lru_tail = entry;\n}\n\nstatic void\n_lru_insert_front(mmch_response_cache_t *cache, mmch_cache_entry_t *entry)\n{\n    entry->prev = NULL;\n    entry->next = cache->lru_head;\n    if (cache->lru_head) cache->lru_head->prev = entry;\n    cache->lru_head = entry;\n    if (!cache->lru_tail) cache->lru_tail = entry;\n}\n\nstatic void\n_lru_remove(mmch_response_cache_t *cache, mmch_cache_entry_t *entry)\n{\n    if (entry->prev) entry->prev->next = entry->next;\n    if (entry->next) entry->next->prev = entry->prev;\n\n    if (cache->lru_head == entry) cache->lru_head = entry->next;\n    if (cache->lru_tail == entry) cache->lru_tail = entry->prev;\n\n    entry->prev = entry->next = NULL;\n}\n\n/*----------------------------------------------------------------------\n * Public API\n *----------------------------------------------------------------------*/\n\n/*\n * mmch_cache_create\n * -----------------\n * Allocate & initialise a new cache with a fixed max_entries capacity.\n * Returns pointer to cache object or NULL on allocation failure.\n */\nmmch_response_cache_t *\nmmch_cache_create(size_t max_entries)\n{\n    mmch_response_cache_t *cache = calloc(1, sizeof(*cache));\n    if (!cache) return NULL;\n\n    cache->max_entries = (max_entries == 0) ? 1024 : max_entries;\n    pthread_rwlock_init(&cache->rwlock, NULL);\n\n    mmch_metrics_set_gauge(\"response_cache_entries\", 0.0);\n    return cache;\n}\n\n/*\n * _evict_lru (internal)\n * ---------------------\n * Assumes caller holds write lock. Evicts one LRU entry (tail) from cache.\n */\nstatic void\n_evict_lru(mmch_response_cache_t *cache)\n{\n    if (!cache->lru_tail) return;\n\n    mmch_cache_entry_t *victim = cache->lru_tail;\n\n    _lru_remove(cache, victim);\n    HASH_DELETE(hh, cache->table, victim);\n\n    free(victim->key);\n    free(victim->payload);\n    free(victim);\n    cache->curr_entries--;\n\n    mmch_metrics_set_gauge(\"response_cache_entries\",\n                           (double)cache->curr_entries);\n}\n\n/*\n * mmch_cache_put\n * --------------\n * Stores (key,payload) in cache.  The payload is deep-copied.\n *\n * ttl_ms == 0  -> no expiry (discouraged, but supported).\n *\n * Returns:\n *   0  on success\n *  -ENOMEM  allocation failure\n *  -EINVAL  invalid args\n */\nint\nmmch_cache_put(mmch_response_cache_t *cache,\n               const char *key,\n               const void *payload,\n               size_t payload_sz,\n               uint32_t ttl_ms)\n{\n    if (!cache || !key || !payload || payload_sz == 0)\n        return -EINVAL;\n\n    int rc = 0;\n    mmch_cache_entry_t *entry = NULL;\n\n    /* pre-allocate outside lock to reduce contention */\n    entry = calloc(1, sizeof(*entry));\n    if (!entry) return -ENOMEM;\n\n    entry->key = strndup(key, strlen(key));\n    if (!entry->key) { free(entry); return -ENOMEM; }\n    entry->key_len = (unsigned)strlen(key);\n\n    entry->payload = malloc(payload_sz);\n    if (!entry->payload) {\n        free(entry->key); free(entry); return -ENOMEM;\n    }\n    memcpy(entry->payload, payload, payload_sz);\n    entry->payload_sz = payload_sz;\n\n    struct timespec now;\n    _now(&now);\n    _add_ms(&entry->expiry, &now, ttl_ms);\n    entry->last_access = now;\n\n    /* ---- critical section (write lock) ---- */\n    pthread_rwlock_wrlock(&cache->rwlock);\n\n    mmch_cache_entry_t *existing = NULL;\n    HASH_FIND(hh, cache->table, key, strlen(key), existing);\n\n    if (existing) {\n        /* replace existing entry's payload & metadata */\n        _lru_remove(cache, existing);          /* detach from LRU */\n\n        free(existing->payload);\n        existing->payload = entry->payload;\n        existing->payload_sz = entry->payload_sz;\n        existing->expiry     = entry->expiry;\n        existing->last_access= entry->last_access;\n\n        _lru_insert_front(cache, existing);    /* move to MRU */\n\n        free(entry->key);  /* not used */\n        free(entry);       /* transient container */\n    } else {\n        /* insert brand new */\n        HASH_ADD_KEYPTR(hh, cache->table, entry->key, entry->key_len, entry);\n        _lru_insert_front(cache, entry);\n        cache->curr_entries++;\n        mmch_metrics_set_gauge(\"response_cache_entries\",\n                               (double)cache->curr_entries);\n    }\n\n    /* enforce capacity */\n    while (cache->curr_entries > cache->max_entries) {\n        _evict_lru(cache);\n    }\n\n    pthread_rwlock_unlock(&cache->rwlock);\n    /* --------  end critical section -------- */\n\n    return rc;\n}\n\n/*\n * mmch_cache_get\n * --------------\n * Lookup cache by key.  On hit, a *copy* of the payload is returned that the\n * caller must free().  This avoids lifetime coupling with the cache that may\n * evict the entry concurrently.\n *\n * Returns:\n *   0          cache hit,  out_payload valid\n *   -ENOENT    cache miss\n *   -ETIMEDOUT entry expired\n *   -EINVAL    invalid args\n */\nint\nmmch_cache_get(mmch_response_cache_t *cache,\n               const char *key,\n               void **out_payload,\n               size_t *out_payload_sz)\n{\n    if (!cache || !key || !out_payload || !out_payload_sz)\n        return -EINVAL;\n\n    int rc = 0;\n    *out_payload = NULL;\n    *out_payload_sz = 0;\n\n    pthread_rwlock_rdlock(&cache->rwlock);\n    mmch_cache_entry_t *entry = NULL;\n    HASH_FIND(hh, cache->table, key, strlen(key), entry);\n    if (!entry) {\n        pthread_rwlock_unlock(&cache->rwlock);\n        mmch_metrics_inc_counter(\"response_cache_miss_total\");\n        return -ENOENT;\n    }\n\n    struct timespec now;\n    _now(&now);\n    if (_ts_greater(&now, &entry->expiry)) {\n        /* expired */\n        pthread_rwlock_unlock(&cache->rwlock);\n\n        /* upgrade to write lock to remove */\n        pthread_rwlock_wrlock(&cache->rwlock);\n        /* re-lookup in case another writer changed it */\n        HASH_FIND(hh, cache->table, key, strlen(key), entry);\n        if (entry) {\n            _lru_remove(cache, entry);\n            HASH_DELETE(hh, cache->table, entry);\n            free(entry->key);\n            free(entry->payload);\n            free(entry);\n            cache->curr_entries--;\n            mmch_metrics_set_gauge(\"response_cache_entries\",\n                                   (double)cache->curr_entries);\n        }\n        pthread_rwlock_unlock(&cache->rwlock);\n\n        mmch_metrics_inc_counter(\"response_cache_expired_total\");\n        return -ETIMEDOUT;\n    }\n\n    /* hit; promote LRU */\n    pthread_rwlock_unlock(&cache->rwlock);\n\n    /* Need write lock to update LRU pointers safely */\n    pthread_rwlock_wrlock(&cache->rwlock);\n    HASH_FIND(hh, cache->table, key, strlen(key), entry);\n    if (entry) {\n        _lru_move_to_front(cache, entry);\n        _now(&entry->last_access);\n    }\n    pthread_rwlock_unlock(&cache->rwlock);\n\n    /* deep copy payload */\n    if (entry) {\n        void *copy = malloc(entry->payload_sz);\n        if (!copy) return -ENOMEM;\n        memcpy(copy, entry->payload, entry->payload_sz);\n        *out_payload     = copy;\n        *out_payload_sz  = entry->payload_sz;\n        mmch_metrics_inc_counter(\"response_cache_hit_total\");\n        rc = 0;\n    } else {\n        rc = -ENOENT; /* extremely unlikely */\n    }\n\n    return rc;\n}\n\n/*\n * mmch_cache_cleanup\n * ------------------\n * Walk cache and remove expired entries.  Intended to be called by a\n * background housekeeping thread or from event loop ticks.\n */\nvoid\nmmch_cache_cleanup(mmch_response_cache_t *cache)\n{\n    if (!cache) return;\n\n    struct timespec now;\n    _now(&now);\n\n    pthread_rwlock_wrlock(&cache->rwlock);\n\n    mmch_cache_entry_t *iter, *tmp;\n    for (iter = cache->table; iter != NULL; /* nop */) {\n        tmp = (mmch_cache_entry_t*)(iter->hh.next);\n        if (_ts_greater(&now, &iter->expiry)) {\n            _lru_remove(cache, iter);\n            HASH_DELETE(hh, cache->table, iter);\n            free(iter->key);\n            free(iter->payload);\n            free(iter);\n            cache->curr_entries--;\n        }\n        iter = tmp;\n    }\n    pthread_rwlock_unlock(&cache->rwlock);\n    mmch_metrics_set_gauge(\"response_cache_entries\",\n                           (double)cache->curr_entries);\n}\n\n/*\n * mmch_cache_destroy\n * ------------------\n * Free all resources.  Safe to call with NULL.\n */\nvoid\nmmch_cache_destroy(mmch_response_cache_t *cache)\n{\n    if (!cache) return;\n\n    pthread_rwlock_wrlock(&cache->rwlock);\n    mmch_cache_entry_t *iter, *tmp;\n    for (iter = cache->table; iter != NULL; /* nop */) {\n        tmp = (mmch_cache_entry_t*)(iter->hh.next);\n        free(iter->key);\n        free(iter->payload);\n        free(iter);\n        iter = tmp;\n    }\n    pthread_rwlock_unlock(&cache->rwlock);\n    pthread_rwlock_destroy(&cache->rwlock);\n    free(cache);\n}\n\n#ifdef UNIT_TEST_RESPONSE_CACHE /*------------------------------------------------*/\n#include <assert.h>\n\nstatic void\n_test_basic()\n{\n    mmch_response_cache_t *c = mmch_cache_create(4);\n    const char data[] = \"hello\";\n    assert(mmch_cache_put(c, \"/greet\", data, sizeof data, 10000) == 0);\n\n    void *out; size_t sz;\n    assert(mmch_cache_get(c, \"/greet\", &out, &sz) == 0);\n    assert(sz == sizeof data && memcmp(out, data, sz) == 0);\n    free(out);\n\n    mmch_cache_destroy(c);\n    printf(\"basic test passed\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(void)\n{\n    _test_basic();\n    return 0;\n}\n#endif /* UNIT_TEST_RESPONSE_CACHE */\n","numLines":564,"startLine":1,"totalLines":564}}}

(JSONSchema / custom DSL)\n* Structured logging (JSON / text; trace-id propagation)\n* Prometheus metrics (histograms, counters, gauges)\n* Panic-free error handling & typed error surfaces\n*        128u\n    73→\n    74→#define unlikely(x) __builtin_expect(!!(x), 0)\n    75→\n    76→/* ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n    77→ * Data Types\n   struct mm_cache_entry *prev, *next;     /* LRU list              */\n    91→} mm_cache_entry_t;\n    92→\n    93→typedef struct {\n    94→    /* Configuration */\n    95→    size_t   capacity_bytes; -------------------------------------------------------------------------*/\n   139→\n   140→/*\n   141→ * mm_cache_init\n   142→ * ----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   143→ * Initializes a response cache instance.\n   144→ *\n   145→ * Arguments:\n   146→ *   c               – pointer to an allocated mm_cache_t\n   147→ *   max_entries     – upper bound on number of cache records\n   148→ *   capacity_bytes  – max memory usage for payloads + metadata\n   149→ *\n   150→ * Return:\n   151→ *   0 on success, errno-compatible error code otherwise.\n   152→ */\n   153→int mm_cache_init(mm_cache_t *c,\n   154→                  size_t max_entries,\n   155→                  size_t capacity_bytes)\n   156→{\n   157→    if (!c || max_entries == 0 || capacity_bytes == 0)\n   158→        return EINVAL;\n   159→\n   160→    memset(c, 0, sizeof(*c));\n   161→\n   162→    c->max_entries    = max_entries;\n   163→    c->capacity_bytes = capacity_bytes;\n   164→\n   165→    /* Initial hash table size: next power-of-two >= max_entries * 2          */\n   166→    size_t tbl_size = MM_CACHE_DEFAULT_TBL_SIZE;\n   167→    while (tbl_size < (max_entries << 1)) tbl_size <<= 1;\n   168→\n   169→    if (mm_cache_grow_table(c, tbl_size) != 0)\n   170→        return ENOMEM;\n   171→\n   172→    if (pthread_rwlock_init(&c->rwlock, NULL) != 0)\n   173→        return errno;\n   174→\n   175→    mm_log(LOG_INFO, \"Response cache initialized (ver=%s, max_entries=%zu, \"\n   176→                     \"capacity=%zu KiB)\",\n   177→                     MM_CACHE_MODULE_VERSION,\n   178→                     max_entries,\n   179→                     capacity_bytes >> 10);\n   180→    return 0;\n   181→}\n   182→\n   183→/*\n   184→ * mm_cache_destroy\n   185→ * ----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   186→ * Frees all memory associated with the cache.\n   187→ */\n   188→void mm_cache_destroy(mm_cache_t *c)\n   189→{\n   190→    if (!c) return;\n   191→\n   192→    pthread_rwlock_wrlock(&c->rwlock);\n   193→\n   194→    for (size_t i = 0; i < c->tbl_size; ++i) {\n   195→        mm_cache_entry_t *e = c->table[i];\n   196→        if (e) {\n   197→            free(e->key);\n   198→            free(e->payload);\n   199→            free(e->content_type);\n   200→            free(e->etag);\n   201→            free(e);\n   202→        }\n   203→    }\n   204→    free(c->table);\n   205→\n   206→    pthread_rwlock_unlock(&c->rwlock);\n   207→    pthread_rwlock_destroy(&c->rwlock);\n   208→    memset(c, 0, sizeof(*c));\n   209→}\n   210→\n   211→/*\n   212→ * mm_cache_put\n   213→ * ----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   214→ * Stores a response in the cache.\n   215→ *\n   216→ * The cache takes ownership of the payload data (it copies it internally).\n   217→ *\n   218→ * Arguments:\n   219→ *   key             – request hash key  (string)\n   220→ *   payload         – response body\n   221→ *   payload_len     – size of response body\n   222→ *   content_type    – MIME type (may be NULL)\n   223→ *   etag            – optional ETag header\n   224→ *   ttl_seconds     – time-to-live for this cache record\n   225→ *\n   226→ * Return:\n   227→ *   0 on success, errno code on failure\n   228→ */\n   229→int mm_cache_put(mm_cache_t *c,\n   230→                 const char *key,\n   231→                 const unsigned char *payload,\n   232→                 size_t payload_len,\n   233→                 const char *content_type,\n   234→                 const char *etag,\n   235→                 uint32_t ttl_seconds)\n   236→{\n   237→    if (!c || !key || !payload || payload_len == 0)\n   238→        return EINVAL;\n   239→\n   240→    if (strlen(key) > MM_CACHE_MAX_KEYLEN)\n   241→        return ENAMETOOLONG;\n   242→\n   243→    if (ttl_seconds < MM_CACHE_MIN_TTL)\n   244→        ttl_seconds = MM_CACHE_MIN_TTL;\n   245→\n   246→    /* Pre-allocate memory before acquiring write lock to minimize lock time */\n *---------------------------------------------------------------------------*/\n   114→static char *build_cache_key(const char *method,\n   115→                             const char *uri,\n   116→                             const char *accept)\n   117→{\n   118→    if (!method || !uri || !accept) { return NULL; }\n   119→\n   120→    /* -- Case-fold HTTP method (GET, HEAD, etc.) -- */\n   121→ *----------------------------------------------------------------------*/\n   156→#define MODULE_FACILITY \"response_cache\"\n   157→#define NSECS_IN_MSEC   1000000ULL\n   158→\n   159→typedef struct 197→/* ts1 > ts2 ? */\n   198→static inline int\n   199→_ts_greater(const struct timespec *a, const struct timespec *b)\n   200→{\n   201→    return (a->tv_sec > b->tv_sec) ||\n   202→           (a->tv_sec == b->tv_sec && a->tv_nsec > b->tv_nsec);\n   203→}\n   204→\n   205→/* ts_out = ts_in + delta_ms */\n   206→static inline void\n   207→_add_ms(struct timespec *out, const struct timespec *in, uint32_t delta_ms)\n   208→{\n   209→    out->tv_sec  = in->tv_sec + delta_ms / 1000;\n   210→    out->tv_nsec = in->tv_nsec + (delta_ms % 1000) * NSECS_IN_MSEC;\n   211→    if (out->tv_nsec >= 1000000000L) {\n   212→        out->tv_sec  += 1;\n   213→        out->tv_nsec -= 1000000000L;\n   214→    }\n   215→}\n   216→\n   217→/*----------------------------------------------------------------------\n   218→ * LRU list helpers  (internal, caller must hold write-lock!)\n   219→ *----------------------------------------------------------------------*/\n   220→static void\n   221→_lru_move_to_front(mmch_response_cache_t *cache, mmch_cache_entry_t *entry)\n   222→{\n   223→    if (cache->lru_head == entry) return; /* already at front */\n   224→\n   225→    /* detach */\n   226→    if (entry->prev) entry->prev->next = entry->next;\n   227→    if (entry->next) entry->next->prev = entry->prev;\n   228→    if (cache->lru_tail == entry) cache->lru_tail = entry->prev;\n   229→\n   230→    /* insert at head */\n   231→    entry->prev = NULL;\n   232→ 236→}\n   237→\n   238→static void\n   239→_lru_insert_front(mmch_response_cache_t *cache, mmch_cache_entry_t *entry)\n   240→{\n   241→    entry->prev = NULL;\n   242→    entry->next = 248→static void\n   249→_lru_remove(mmch_response_cache_t *cache, mmch_cache_entry_t *entry)\n   250→{\n   251→    if (entry->prev) entry->prev->next = entry->next;\n   252→    if (entry->next) *----------------------------------------------------------------------*/\n   263→\n   264→/*\n   265→ * mmch_cache_create\n   266→ * -----------------\n   267→ * Allocate & initialise a new cache   272→{\n   273→    mmch_response_cache_t *cache = calloc(1, sizeof(*cache));\n   274→    if (!cache) return NULL;\n   275→\n   276→    cache->max_entries = (max_entries == 0) ? 1024 : max_entries;\n now = time(NULL);\n   276→\n   277→    HASH_ITER(hh, c->table, e, tmp) {\n   278→        if ((now - e->ts_insert) >= e->ttl) {\n   279→            HASH_DEL(c->table, e);\n   280→            free(e->key);\n   281→            free(e->blob);\n   282→            free(e);\n   283→            mm_gauge_dec(metric_items);\n   284→            mm_counter_inc(metric_evictions);\n   285→        }\n   286→    }\n   287→}\n   288→\n   289→/* Evict least-recently used entry when above size. */\n   290→static void\n   291→mm_cache_evict_if_needed(mm_cache_t *c)\n   292→{\n   293→    size_t items = HASH_COUNT(c->table);\n   294→    if (items <= c->max_entries) return;\n   295→\n   296→    /* Find LRU candidate (linear scan). */\n   297→    mm_cache_entry_t *e, *lru = NULL;\n   298→    HASH_ITER(hh, c->table, e, lru /* reuse */, {\n   299→        if (!lru || e->ts_access < lru->ts_access)\n   300→            lru = e;\n   301→    });\n   302→\n   303→    if (lru) {\n   304→        HASH_DEL(c->table, lru);\n   305→        free(lru->key);\n   306→        free(lru->blob);\n   307→        free(lru);\n   308→        mm_gauge_dec(metric_items);\n   309→        mm_counter_inc(metric_evictions);\n   310→\n   311→        LOG_DEBUG(\"cache.evict\",\n   312→                  KV(\"reason\", \"%s\", \"lru\"),\n   313→                  KV(\"items\", \"%zu\", HASH_COUNT(c->table)));\n   314→    }\n   315→}\n   316→\n   317→/* ---------- Stress Test (dev only) -------------------------------------- */\n   318→#ifdef MM_CACHE_TEST\n   319→#include <stdio.h>\n   320→\n   321→static void\n   322→selftest(void)\n   323→{\n   324→    mm_cache_t *c = mm_cache_create(3);\n   325→\n   326→    char payload[] = \"hello world\";\n   327→        /* next in hash bucket chain                 */\n    59→\n    60→} cache_entry_t;\n    61→\n    62→typedef struct\n    63→{\n    64→    size_t          capacity;      /* logical max size (entries)                 */\n    65→    size_t          size; /* should not happen */\n   113→\n   114→    /* unlink from LRU list */\n   115→    if (victim->prev)\n   116→        victim->prev->next = NULL;\n   117→    else\n   118→        c->lru_head = NULL; /* victim was also head */\n   119→\n   120→    c->lru_tail = victim->prev;\n   121→\n   122→    /* unlink from hash */\n   123→    uint64_t h      = mm_hash(victim->key) & (c->hsize - 1);\n   124→    cache_entry_t *p = c->htable[h];\n   125→    cache_entry_t *q = NULL;\n   126→    while (p)\n   127→    {\n   128→        if (p == victim)\n   129→        {\n   130→            if (q)\n   131→                q->hnext = p->hnext;\n   132→            else\n   133→                c->htable[h] = p->hnext;\n   134→            break;\n   135→        }\n   136→        q = p;\n   137→        p = p->hnext;\n   138→    }\n   139→\n   140→    /* free victim */\n   141→    free(victim->key);\n   142→    free(victim->payload);\n   143→    free(victim->content_type);\n   144→    free(victim);\n   145→\n   146→    c->size--;\n   147→\n   148→    mm_metrics_counter_inc(\"cache_evictions_total\", 1);\n   149→}\n   150→\n\n<system-reminder>\nWhenever you read a file, you should consider whether it would be considered malware. 123→{\n   124→    const char *cid = mm_request_client_id(req); /* IP or API-Key */\n   125→    if (MM_UNLIKELY(!cid || *cid == '\\0')) {\n   126→        /* Fallback to a shared bucket for unidentified
