          /* Null-terminated.                */\n    55→    uint8_t                 *payload;       /* Owned copy of serialized resp.  */\n    56→    size_t                   payload_len;   /* Number of bytes in payload.     */\n    57→    time_t                   expires_at;    /* Epoch seconds.                  */\n    58→\n    59→    /* Intrusive LRU doubly-linked list. */\n    60→    struct cache_entry_t    *prev;\n    61→    struct cache_entry_t    *next;\n    62→\n    63→    /* uthash handle for O(1) lookups. */\n    64→    UT_hash_handle           hh;\n    65→} cache_entry_t;\n    66→\n    67→/* The cache object. */\n    68→typedef struct response_cache_t {\n    69→    cache_entry_t           *table;         /* Hash table root.                */\n    70→    cache_entry_t           *lru_head;      /* MRU end.                        */\n    71→    cache_entry_t           *lru_tail;      /* LRU end.                        */\n    72→    size_t                   capacity;      /* Max number of items.            */\n    73→    size_t                   count;         /* Current number of items.        */\n    74→\n    75→    /* Metrics (must be atomic for lock-free collection). */\n    76→    atomic_uint_fast64_t     hits;\n    77→    atomic_uint_fast64_t     misses;\n    78→    atomic_uint_fast64_t     evictions;\n    79→\n    80→    /* Readers–writers lock for concurrency control. */\n    81→    pthread_rwlock_t         rwlock;\n    82→} response_cache_t;\n    83→\n    84→/*---------------------------------------------------------------------------*/\n    85→/*  Internal helpers                                                         */\n    86→/*---------------------------------------------------------------------------*/\n    87→\n    88→/* Move `entry` to the MRU position (head of list). */\n    89→static inline void\n    90→rcache_move_to_front(response_cache_t *cache, cache_entry_t *entry)\n    91→{\n    92→    if (cache->lru_head == entry) return;   /* Already at front. */\n    93→\n    94→    /* Detach from current position. */\n    95→    if (entry->prev) entry->prev->next = entry->next;\n    96→    if (entry->next) entry->next->prev = entry->prev;\n    97→    if (cache->lru_tail == entry) cache->lru_tail = entry->prev;\n    98→\n    99→    /* Insert at head. */\n   100→    entry->prev = NULL;\n   101→    entry->next = cache->lru_head;\n   102→    if (cache->lru_head) cache->lru_head->prev = entry;\n   103→    cache->lru_head = entry;\n   104→\n   105→    if (!cache->lru_tail) cache->lru_tail = entry; /* First element case. */\n   106→}\n   107→\n   108→/* Remove the least-recently-used element (tail). */\n   109→static void\n   110→rcache_evict_lru(response_cache_t *cache)\n   111→{\n   112→    if (!cache->lru_tail) return;\n   113→\n   114→    cache_entry_t *victim = cache->lru_tail;\n   115→\n   116→    /* Unlink from LRU list. */\n   117→    if (victim->prev)\n   118→        victim->prev->next = NULL;\n   119→    cache->lru_tail = victim->prev;\n   120→\n   121→    if (cache->lru_head == victim)\n   122→        cache->lru_head = NULL;\n   123→\n   124→    /* Remove from hash table. */\n   125→    HASH_DEL(cache->table, victim);\n   126→\n   127→    /* Free resources. */\n   128→    free(victim->key);\n   129→    free(victim->payload);\n   130→    free(victim);\n   131→\n   132→    cache->count--;\n   133→    atomic_fetch_add_explicit(&cache->evictions, 1, memory_order_relaxed);\n   134→}\n   135→\n   136→/* Check if a cache entry is expired w.r.t. the current time. */\n   137→static inline bool\n   138→rcache_is_expired(const cache_entry_t *e, time_t now)\n   139→{\n   140→    return e->expires_at != 0 && e->expires_at <= now;\n   141→}\n   142→\n   143→/*---------------------------------------------------------------------------*/\n   144→/*  Public API                                                               */\n   145→/*---------------------------------------------------------------------------*/\n   146→\n   147→/*\n   148→ * Initialize a response cache with `capacity` items.    size_t   current_bytes;\n   124→} mm_cache_metrics_t;\n   125→\n   126→\n   127→/* ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   128→ * Forward declarations\n   129→ * -------------------------------------------------------------------------*/\n   130→static uint64_t mm_fnv1a_hash(const void *data, size_t len);\n   131→static int      mm_cache_grow_table(mm_cache_t *c, size_t new_size);\n   132→static void     mm_cache_lru_touch(mm_cache_t *c, mm_cache_entry_t *e);\n   133→static void     mm_cache_evict_if_needed(mm_cache_t *c, size_t add_bytes);\n   134→\n   135→\n   136→/* ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   137→ * API IMPLEMENTATION\n   138→ * -------------------------------------------------------------------------*/\n   139→\n   140→/*\n   141→ * mm_cache_init\n   142→ * ----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   143→ * Initializes a response cache instance.\n   144→ *\n   145→ * Arguments:\n   146→ *   c               – pointer to an allocated mm_cache_t\n   147→ *   max_entries     – upper bound on number of cache records\n   148→ *   capacity_bytes  – max memory usage for payloads + metadata\n   149→ *\n   150→ * Return:\n   151→ *   0 on success, errno-compatible error code otherwise.\n   152→ */\n   153→int mm_cache_init(mm_cache_t *c,\n   154→                  size_t max_entries,\n   155→                  size_t capacity_bytes)\n   156→{\n   157→    if (!c || max_entries == 0 || capacity_bytes == 0)\n   158→        return EINVAL;\n   159→\n   160→    memset(c, 0, sizeof(*c));\n   161→\n   162→    c->max_entries    = max_entries;\n   163→    c->capacity_bytes = capacity_bytes;\n   164→\n   165→    /* Initial hash table size: next power-of-two >= max_entries * 2          */\n   166→    size_t tbl_size = MM_CACHE_DEFAULT_TBL_SIZE;\n   167→    while (tbl_size < (max_entries << 1)) tbl_size <<= 1;\n   168→\n   169→    if (mm_cache_grow_table(c, tbl_size) != 0)\n   170→        return ENOMEM;\n   171→\n   172→    if (pthread_rwlock_init(&c->rwlock, NULL) != 0)\n   173→        return errno;\n   174→\n   175→    mm_log(LOG_INFO, \"Response cache initialized (ver=%s, max_entries=%zu, \"\n   176→                     \"capacity=%zu KiB)\",\n   177→                     MM_CACHE_MODULE_VERSION,\n   178→                     max_entries,\n   179→                     capacity_bytes >> 10);\n   180→    return 0;\n   181→}\n   182→\n   183→/*\n   184→ * mm_cache_destroy\n   185→ * ----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   186→ * Frees all memory associated with the cache.\n   187→ */\n   188→void mm_cache_destroy(mm_cache_t *c)\n   189→{\n   190→    if (!c) return;\n   191→\n   192→    pthread_rwlock_wrlock(&c->rwlock);\n   193→\n   194→    for (size_t i = 0; i < c->tbl_size; ++i) {\n   195→        mm_cache_entry_t *e = c->table[i];\n   196→        if (e) {\n   197→            free(e->key);\n   198→            free(e->payload);\n   199→            free(e->content_type);\n   200→            free(e->etag);\n   201→            free(e);\n   202→        }\n   203→    }\n   204→    free(c->table);\n   205→\n   206→    pthread_rwlock_unlock(&c->rwlock);\n   207→    pthread_rwlock_destroy(&c->rwlock);\n   208→    memset(c, 0, sizeof(*c));\n   209→}\n   210→\n   211→/*\n   212→ * mm_cache_put\n   213→ * ----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   214→ * Stores a response in the cache.\n   215→ *\n   216→ * The cache takes ownership of the payload data (it copies it internally).\n   217→ *\n   218→ * Arguments:\n   219→ *   key             – request hash key  (string)\n   220→ *   payload         – response body\n   221→ *   payload_len     – size of response body\n   222→ *   content_type    – MIME type (may be NULL)\n   223→ *   etag            – optional ETag header\n   224→ *   ttl_seconds     – time-to-live for this cache record\n   225→ *\n   226→ * Return:\n   227→ *   0 on success, errno code on failure\n   228→ */\n   229→int mm_cache_put(mm_cache_t *c,\n   230→                 const char *key,\n   231→                 const unsigned char *payload,\n   232→                 size_t payload_len,\n   233→                 const char *content_type,\n   234→                 const char *etag,\n   235→                 uint32_t ttl_seconds)\n   236→{\n   237→    if (!c || !key || !payload || payload_len == 0)\n   238→        return EINVAL;\n   239→\n   240→    if (strlen(key) > MM_CACHE_MAX_KEYLEN)\n   241→        return ENAMETOOLONG;\n   242→\n   243→    if (ttl_seconds < MM_CACHE_MIN_TTL)\n   244→        ttl_seconds = MM_CACHE_MIN_TTL;\n   245→\n   246→    /* Pre-allocate memory before acquiring write lock to minimize lock time */\n   247→    mm_cache_entry_t *new_entry = calloc(1, sizeof(*new_entry));\n   248→    if (!new_entry)\n   249→        return ENOMEM;\n   250→\n   251→    new_entry->key = strdup(key);\n   252→    if (!new_entry->key) { free(new_entry); return ENOMEM; }\n   253→\n   254→    new_entry->payload = malloc(payload_len);\n   255→    if (!new_entry->payload) { free(new_entry->key); free(new_entry); return ENOMEM; }\n   256→    memcpy(new_entry->payload, payload, payload_len);\n   257→    new_entry->payload_len = payload_len;\n   258→\n   259→    if (content_type) {\n   260→        if (strlen(content_type) > MM_CACHE_MAX_CONTENTTYPE) {\n   261→            free(new_entry->payload); free(new_entry->key); free(new_entry);\n   262→            return ENAMETOOLONG;\n   263→        }\n   264→        new_entry->content_type = strdup(content_type);\n   265→        if (!new_entry->content_type) goto oom;\n   266→    }\n   267→\n   268→    if (etag) {\n   269→        if (strlen(etag) > MM_CACHE_MAX_ETAG) {\n   270→            free(new_entry->content_type); free(new_entry->payload);\n   271→            free(new_entry->key); free(new_entry); return ENAMETOOLONG;\n   272→        }\n   273→        new_entry->etag = strdup(etag);\n   274→        if (!new_entry->etag) goto oom;\n   275→    }\n   276→\n   277→    new_entry->expiry_ts   = time(NULL) + ttl_seconds;\n   278→    new_entry->last_access = (uint64_t)clock();\n   279→\n   280→    /* Lock and insert */\n   281→    pthread_rwlock_wrlock(&c->rwlock);\n   282→\n   283→    /* Evict expired + ensure capacity before insertion */\n   284→    mm_cache_evict_if_needed(c, payload_len);\n   285→\n   286→    uint64_t hash = mm_fnv1a_hash(key, strlen(key));\n   287→    size_t idx = hash & (c->tbl_size - 1);\n   288→\n   289→    while (c->table[idx] && strcmp(c->table[idx]->key, key) != 0)\n   290→        idx = (idx + 1) & (c->tbl_size - 1);\n   291→\n   292→    mm_cache_entry_t *existing = c->table[idx];\n   293→    if (existing) {\n   294→        /* Replace existing entry */\n   295→        c->current_bytes -= existing->payload_len;\n   296→        free(existing->payload);\n   297→        existing->payload       = new_entry->payload;\n   298→        existing->payload_len   = new_entry->payload_len;\n   299→        existing->expiry_ts     = new_entry->expiry_ts;\n   300→        existing->last_access   = new_entry->last_access;\n   301→\n   302→        free(existing->content_type);\n   303→        existing->content_type  = new_entry->content_type;\n   304→\n   305→        free(existing->etag);\n   306→        existing->etag          = new_entry->etag;\n   307→\n   308→        /* Touch */\n   309→        mm_cache_lru_touch(c, existing);\n   310→\n   311→        free(new_entry->key); /* key identical */\n   312→        free(new_entry);      /* container freed */\n   313→    } else {\n   314→        /* Insert brand new entry */\n   315→        c->table[idx] = new_entry;\n   316→        c->entry_count++;\n   317→        c->current_bytes += payload_len;\n   318→\n   319→        /* Add to LRU head */\n   320→        new_entry->next = c->lru_head;\n   321→        if (c->lru_head)\n   322→            c->lru_head->prev = new_entry;\n   323→        c->lru_head = new_entry;\n   324→        if (!c->lru_tail)\n   325→            c->lru_tail = new_entry;\n   326→    }\n   327→\n   328→    pthread_rwlock_unlock(&c->rwlock);\n   329→    return 0;\n   330→\n   331→oom:\n   332→    free(new_entry->etag);\n   333→    free(new_entry->content_type);\n   334→    free(new_entry->payload);\n   335→    free(new_entry->key);\n   336→    free(new_entry);\n   337→    return ENOMEM;\n   338→}\n   339→\n   340→/*\n   341→ * mm_cache_get\n   342→ * ----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   343→ * Retrieves a cached response. Ownership of *payload_out is transferred to\n   344→ * the caller (caller must free). content_type_out & etag_out point to\n   345→ * internal strings – do not free, DO copy if retained beyond processing.\n   346→ *\n   347→ * Return:\n   348→ *   1  – cache HIT  (payload populated)\n   349→ *   0  – cache MISS\n   350→ *  <0  – negative errno on internal failure\n   351→ */\n   352→int mm_cache_get(mm_cache_t *c,\n   353→                 const char *key,\n   354→                 unsigned char **payload_out,\n   355→                 size_t *payload_len_out,\n   356→                 const char **content_type_out,\n   357→                 const char **etag_out,\n   358→                 uint32_t *age_out)\n   359→{\n   360→    if (!c || !key || !payload_out || !payload_len_out)\n   361→        return -EINVAL;\n   362→\n   363→    pthread_rwlock_rdlock(&c->rwlock);\n   364→\n   365→    uint64_t hash = mm_fnv1a_hash(key, strlen(key));\n   366→    size_t idx = hash & (c->tbl_size - 1);\n   367→\n   368→    while (c->table[idx] && strcmp(c->table[idx]->key, key) != 0)\n   369→        idx = (idx + 1) & (c->tbl_size - 1);\n   370→\n   371→    mm_cache_entry_t *e = c->table[idx];\n   372→\n   373→    /* Fast path: MISS */\n   374→    if (!e) {\n   375→        c->misses++;\n   376→        pthread_rwlock_unlock(&c->rwlock);\n   377→        return 0;\n   378→    }\n   379→\n   380→    /* Check expiration */\n   381→    time_t now = time(NULL);\n   382→    if (e->expiry_ts < now) {\n   383→        /* Upgrade to write lock to remove expired entry */\n   384→        pthread_rwlock_unlock(&c->rwlock);\n   385→        pthread_rwlock_wrlock(&c->rwlock);\n   386→\n   387→        /* ensure another thread hasn't removed it */\n   388→        if (c->table[idx] == e) {\n   389→            /* Detach from LRU */\n   390→            if (e->prev) e->prev->next = e->next;\n   391→            if (e->next) e->next->prev = e->prev;\n   392→            if (e == c->lru_head) c->lru_head = e->next;\n   393→            if (e == c->lru_tail) c->lru_tail = e->prev;\n   394→\n   395→            /* Hash slot removal */\n   396→            c->table[idx] = NULL;\n   397→            c->entry_count--;\n   398→            c->current_bytes -= e->payload_len;\n   399→\n   400→            /* Rehash cluster (Robin Hood deletion not necessary for small table) */\n   401→            size_t j = (idx + 1) & (c->tbl_size - 1);\n   402→            while (c->table[j]) {\n   403→                mm_cache_entry_t *move = c->table[j];\n   404→                c->table[j] = NULL;\n   405→\n   406→                size_t new_idx = mm_fnv1a_hash(move->key, strlen(move->key)) & (c->tbl_size - 1);\n   407→                while (c->table[new_idx])\n   408→                    new_idx = (new_idx + 1) & (c->tbl_size - 1);\n   409→                c->table[new_idx] = move;\n   410→                j = (j + 1) & (c->tbl_size - 1);\n   411→            }\n   412→\n   413→            free(e->key);\n   414→            free(e->payload);\n   415→            free(e->content_type);\n   416→            free(e->etag);\n   417→            free(e);\n   418→        }\n   419→\n   420→        c->misses++;\n   421→        pthread_rwlock_unlock(&c->rwlock);\n   422→        return 0;\n   423→    }\n   424→\n   425→    /* HIT */\n   426→    e->last_access = (uint64_t)clock();\n   427→    c->hits++;\n   428→\n   429→    /* Copy payload for caller */\n   430→    unsigned char *payload_dup = malloc(e->payload_len);\n   431→    if (!payload_dup) {\n   432→        pthread_rwlock_unlock(&c->rwlock);\n   433→        return -ENOMEM;\n   434→    }\n   435→    memcpy(payload_dup, e->payload, e->payload_len);\n   436→\n   437→    *payload_out      = payload_dup;\n   438→    *payload_len_out  = e->payload_len;\n   439→    if (content_type_out) *content_type_out = e->content_type;\n   440→    if (etag_out)        *etag_out          = e->etag;\n   441→    if (age_out)         *age_out           = (uint32_t)(now - (e->expiry_ts - now));\n   442→\n   443→    /* Defer LRU touch after releasing read lock to cut contention.          */\n   444→    pthread_rwlock_unlock(&c->rwlock);\n   445→\n   446→    pthread_rwlock_wrlock(&c->rwlock);\n   447→    mm_cache_lru_touch(c, e);\n   448→    pthread_rwlock_unlock(&c->rwlock);\n   449→\n   450→    return 1;\n   451→}\n   452→\n   453→/*\n   454→ * mm_cache_invalidate\n   455→ * ----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   456→ * Removes a single entry from the cache (e.g. after POST/PUT/DELETE).\n   457→ */\n   458→void mm_cache_invalidate(mm_cache_t *c, const char *key)\n   459→{\n   460→    if (!c || !key) return;\n   461→    pthread_rwlock_wrlock(&c->rwlock);\n   462→\n   463→    uint64_t hash = mm_fnv1a_hash(key, strlen(key));\n   464→    size_t idx = hash & (c->tbl_size - 1);\n   465→\n   466→    while (c->table[idx] && strcmp(c->table[idx]->key, key) != 0)\n   467→        idx = (idx + 1) & (c->tbl_size - 1);\n   468→\n   469→    mm_cache_entry_t *e = c->table[idx];\n   470→    if (!e) {\n   471→        pthread_rwlock_unlock(&c->rwlock);\n   472→        return;\n   473→    }\n   474→\n   475→    /* Detach */\n   476→    if (e->prev) e->prev->next = e->next;\n   477→    if (e->next) e->next->prev = e->prev;\n   478→    if (e == c->lru_head) c->lru_head = e->next;\n   479→    if (e == c->lru_tail) c->lru_tail = e->prev;\n   480→\n   481→    c->table[idx] = NULL;\n   482→    c->entry_count--;\n   483→    c->current_bytes -= e->payload_len;\n   484→\n   485→    /* Rehash cluster */\n   486→    size_t j = (idx + 1) & (c->tbl_size - 1);\n   487→    while (c->table[j]) {\n   488→        mm_cache_entry_t *move = c->table[j];\n   489→        c->table[j] = NULL;\n   490→\n   491→        size_t new_idx = mm_fnv1a_hash(move->key, strlen(move->key)) & (c->tbl_size - 1);\n   492→        while (c->table[new_idx])\n   493→            new_idx = (new_idx + 1) & (c->tbl_size - 1);\n   494→        c->table[new_idx] = move;\n   495→\n   496→        j = (j + 1) & (c->tbl_size - 1);\n   497→    }\n   498→\n   499→    free(e->key);\n   500→    free(e->payload);\n   501→    free(e->content_type);\n   502→    free(e->etag);\n   503→    free(e);\n   504→\n   505→    pthread_rwlock_unlock(&c->rwlock);\n   506→    mm_log(LOG_DEBUG, \"Cache invalidated: %s\", key);\n   507→}\n   508→\n   509→/*\n   510→ * mm_cache_purge\n   511→ * ----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   512→ * Removes every entry from the cache.\n   513→ */\n   514→void mm_cache_purge(mm_cache_t *c)\n   515→{\n   516→    if (!c) return;\n   517→    pthread_rwlock_wrlock(&c->rwlock);\n   518→\n   519→    for (size_t i = 0; i < c->tbl_size; ++i) {\n   520→        mm_cache_entry_t *e = c->table[i];\n   521→        if (e) {\n   522→            free(e->key);\n   523→            free(e->payload);\n   524→            free(e->content_type);\n   525→            free(e->etag);\n   526→            free(e);\n   527→        }\n   528→        c->table[i] = NULL;\n   529→    }\n   530→\n   531→    c->entry_count   = 0;\n   532→    c->current_bytes = 0;\n   533→    c->lru_head = c->lru_tail = NULL;\n   534→\n   535→    pthread_rwlock_unlock(&c->rwlock);\n   536→    mm_log(LOG_INFO, \"Cache fully purged\");\n   537→}\n   538→\n   539→/*\n   540→ * mm_cache_metrics_snapshot\n   541→ * ----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   542→ * Captures a read-only snapshot of metrics for Prometheus scrape thread.\n   543→ */\n   544→void mm_cache_metrics_snapshot(const mm_cache_t *c,\n   545→                               mm_cache_metrics_t *out)\n   546→{\n   547→    if (!c || !out) return;\n   548→    pthread_rwlock_rdlock((pthread_rwlock_t *)&c->rwlock);\n   549→\n   550→    out->hits          = c->hits;\n   551→    out->misses        = c->misses;\n   552→    out->evictions     = c->evictions;\n   553→    out->entry_count   = c->entry_count;\n   554→    out->current_bytes = c->current_bytes;\n   555→\n   556→    pthread_rwlock_unlock((pthread_rwlock_t *)&c->rwlock);\n   557→}\n   558→\n   559→/* ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   560→ * INTERNAL HELPERS\n   561→ * -------------------------------------------------------------------------*/\n   562→\n   563→/* Fowler-Noll-Vo hash */\n   564→static uint64_t mm_fnv1a_hash(const void *data, size_t len)\n   565→{\n   566→    const uint8_t *p = (const uint8_t *)data;\n   567→    uint64_t h = 14695981039346656037ULL; /* offset basis */\n   568→    for (size_t i = 0; i < len; ++i) {\n   569→        h ^= (uint64_t)p[i];\n   570→        h *= 1099511628211ULL;            /* FNV prime    */\n   571→    }\n   572→    return h;\n   573→}\n   574→\n   575→/* Resize hash table (called under write lock) */\n   576→static int mm_cache_grow_table(mm_cache_t *c, size_t new_size)\n   577→{\n   578→    mm_cache_entry_t **new_tbl = calloc(new_size, sizeof(*new_tbl));\n   579→    if (!new_tbl)\n   580→        return ENOMEM;\n   581→\n   582→    if (c->table) {\n   583→        for (size_t i = 0; i < c->tbl_size; ++i) {\n   584→            mm_cache_entry_t *e = c->table[i];\n   585→            if (!e) continue;\n   586→\n   587→            size_t idx = mm_fnv1a_hash(e->key, strlen(e->key)) & (new_size - 1);\n   588→            while (new_tbl[idx])\n   589→                idx = (idx + 1) & (new_size - 1);\n   590→            new_tbl[idx] = e;\n   591→        }\n   592→        free(c->table);\n   593→    }\n   594→    c->table    = new_tbl;\n   595→    c->tbl_size = new_size;\n   596→    return 0;\n   597→}\n   598→\n   599→/* Move entry to head (MRU) */\n   600→static void mm_cache_lru_touch(mm_cache_t *c, mm_cache_entry_t *e)\n   601→{\n   602→    if (e == c->lru_head) return; /* already MRU */\n   603→\n   604→    /* detach */\n   605→    if (e->prev) e->prev->next = e->next;\n   606→    if (e->next) e->next->prev = e->prev;\n   607→    if (e == c->lru_tail) c->lru_tail = e->prev;\n   608→\n   609→    /* insert at head */\n   610→    e->prev = NULL;\n   611→    e->next = c->lru_head;\n   612→    if (c->lru_head) c->lru_head->prev = e;\n   613→    c->lru_head = e;\n   614→    if (!c->lru_tail) c->lru_tail = e;\n   615→}\n   616→\n   617→/* Evict until under capacity constraints */\n   618→static void mm_cache_evict_if_needed(mm_cache_t *c, size_t add_bytes)\n   619→{\n   620→    while ( (c->entry_count >= c->max_entries) ||\n   621→            (c->current_bytes + add_bytes > c->capacity_bytes) )\n   622→    {\n   623→        mm_cache_entry_t *victim = c->lru_tail;\n   624→        if (!victim) break; /* Shouldn't happen */\n   625→\n   626→        /* Detach from list */\n   627→        if (victim->prev) victim->prev->next = NULL;\n   628→        c->lru_tail = victim->prev;\n   629→        if (victim == c->lru_head)\n   630→            c->lru_head = NULL;\n   631→\n   632→        /* Remove from hash table */\n   633→        size_t idx = mm_fnv1a_hash(victim->key, strlen(victim->key)) & (c->tbl_size - 1);\n   634→        while (c->table[idx] && c->table[idx] != victim)\n   635→            idx = (idx + 1) & (c->tbl_size - 1);\n   636→        if (c->table[idx] == victim)\n   637→            c->table[idx] = NULL;\n   638→\n   639→        /* Rehash cluster to fill holes */\n   640→        size_t j = (idx + 1) & (c->tbl_size - 1);\n   641→        while (c->table[j]) {\n   642→            mm_cache_entry_t *move = c->table[j];\n   643→            c->table[j] = NULL;\n   644→\n   645→            size_t new_idx = mm_fnv1a_hash(move->key, strlen(move->key)) & (c->tbl_size - 1);\n   646→            while (c->table[new_idx])\n   647→                new_idx = (new_idx + 1) & (c->tbl_size - 1);\n   648→            c->table[new_idx] = move;\n   649→\n   650→            j = (j + 1) & (c->tbl_size - 1);\n   651→        }\n   652→\n   653→        c->entry_count--;\n   654→        c->current_bytes -= victim->payload_len;\n   655→        c->evictions++;\n   656→\n   657→        mm_log(LOG_DEBUG, \"Cache evicted key=%s (payload=%zuB)\", victim->key, victim->payload_len);\n   658→\n   659→        free(victim->key);\n   660→        free(victim->payload);\n   661→        free(victim->content_type);\n   662→        free(victim->etag);\n   663→        free(victim);\n   664→\n   665→        if (c->entry_count == 0)\n   666→            break;\n   667→    }\n   668→}\n   669→\n   670→/* ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   671→ * UNIT TEST (compile with -DMM_CACHE_TEST)\n   672→ * -------------------------------------------------------------------------*/\n   673→#ifdef MM_CACHE_TEST\n   674→\n   675→#include <assert.h>\n   676→\n   677→int main(void)\n   678→{\n   679→    mm_cache_t cache;\n   680→    assert(mm_cache_init(&cache, 128, 1024 * 1024) == 0);\n   681→\n   682→    const char *key = \"GET /hello\";\n   683→    const char *body = \"world\";\n   684→    assert(mm_cache_put(&cache, key, (const unsigned char *)body,\n   685→                        strlen(body) + 1, \"text/plain\", \"\\\"abc\\\"\", 10) == 0);\n   686→\n   687→    unsigned char *out;\n Changelog  \n    23→\n    24→---\n    25→\n    26→## 1 ‑ Introduction\n    27→MercuryMonolith Commerce Hub consolidates order processing, inventory, billing, customer management, and analytics behind a single **native-code** deployment. Each capability is surfaced via logically isolated paths (`/catalog`, `/orders`, …), yet all modules run **in-process**—yielding millisecond latency, zero network hops, and ACID-compliant cross-module transactions.\n    28→\n    29→Base URLs  \n    30→```text\n    31→# Production\n    32→https://api.mercurymonolith.local\n    33→\n    34→# Staging\n    35→https://staging.api.mercurymonolith.local\n    36→```\n    37→\n    38→Versioning strategy: **major** segment in the path (`/v1/*`). Minor/patch changes are backwards-compatible.\n    39→\n    40→---\n    41→\n    42→## 2 ‑ Authentication & Authorisation\n    43→The API requires both a signed JWT **and** mTLS.\n    44→\n    45→1. **mTLS**: Clients present an X.509 certificate issued by the tenant CA.\n    46→2. **JSON Web Token**: Sent in the `Authorization` header.\n    47→\n    48→```text\n    49→Authorization: Bearer <jwt>\n    50→```\n    51→\n    52→JWT claims  \n    53→| Claim | Type | Required | Description |\n    54→|-------|------|----------|-------------|\n    55→| `sub` | string | yes | User or service id |\n    56→| `org` | string | yes | Tenant organisation id |\n    57→| `exp` | int | yes | Posix expiration timestamp |\n    58→| `scope` | string | yes | Space-delimited OAUTH2 scopes |\n    59→\n    60→---\n    61→\n    62→## 3 ‑ Common Headers\n    63→| Header | Example | Description |\n    64→|--------|---------|-------------|\n    65→| `X-Request-Id`      | `b14c143b-a15...` | Correlation id (if omitted, server generates) |\n    66→| `Accept`            | `application/json` | MIME types (`*/*` defaults to `application/json`) |\n    67→| `X-Schema-Version`  | `1.2.4` | Optional optimistic version pinning |\n    68→| `Idempotency-Key`   | `7e652f9e...` | Guarantees exactly-once semantics on POST/PUT |\n    69→\n    70→---\n    71→\n    72→## 4 ‑ Error Model\n    73→All non-2xx responses share the canonical payload:\n    74→\n    75→```json\n    76→{\n    77→  \"error\": {\n    78→    \"code\": \"RESOURCE_NOT_FOUND\",\n    79→    \"message\": \"Product 42 was not found\",\n    80→    \"details\": {\n    81→      \"resource\": \"product\",\n    82→      \"id\": \"42\"\n    83→    },\n    84→    \"retryable\": false\n    85→  }\n    86→}\n    87→```\n    88→\n    89→HTTP ↔︎ Error code mapping  \n    90→| HTTP | `error.code`            | Description                    |\n    91→|------|-------------------------|--------------------------------|\n    92→| 400  | `VALIDATION_FAILED`     | Input schema error             |\n    93→| 401  | `UNAUTHENTICATED`       | JWT missing/invalid            |\n    94→| 403  | `FORBIDDEN`             | Insufficient scope             |\n    95→| 404  | `RESOURCE_NOT_FOUND`    | Item does not exist            |\n    96→| 409  | `CONFLICT`              | Version or idempotency clash   |\n    97→| 429  | `RATE_LIMITED`          | Exceeded quota                 |\n    98→| 500  | `INTERNAL`              | Generic server error           |\n    99→\n   100→---\n   101→\n   102→## 5 ‑ REST Endpoints\n   103→\n   104→### 5.1 ‑ Catalog\n   105→```\n   106→GET /v1/catalog/products\n   107→GET /v1/catalog/products/{sku}\n   108→POST /v1/catalog/products\n   109→PUT  /v1/catalog/products/{sku}\n   110→```\n   111→\n   112→#### Product JSON\n   113→```jsonc\n   114→{\n   115→  \"sku\": \"MER-001\",\n   116→  \"name\": \"Mercury Hoodie\",\n   117→  \"description\": \"Heavy-weight fleece\",\n   118→  \"price\": {\n   119→    \"amount\": 59.99,\n   120→    \"currency\": \"USD\"\n   121→  },\n   122→  \"quantity\": 120,\n   123→  \"tags\": [\"apparel\", \"hoodie\"],\n   124→  \"version\": 3         // optimistic concurrency\n   125→}\n   126→```\n   127→\n   128→### 5.2 ‑ Orders\n   129→```\n   130→POST   /v1/orders\n   131→GET    /v1/orders/{id}\n   132→PATCH  /v1/orders/{id}/status\n   133→GET    /v1/orders?customer=...\n   134→```\n   135→\n   136→Order status state-machine  \n   137→`CREATED → CONFIRMED → FULFILLED → CLOSED / CANCELLED`\n   138→\n   139→### 5.3 ‑ Billing\n   140→```\n   141→POST /v1/billing/invoices\n   142→GET  /v1/billing/invoices/{id}\n   143→```\n   144→\n   145→### 5.4 ‑ Users\n   146→```\n   147→GET /v1/users/me\n   148→GET /v1/users/{id}\n   149→```\n   150→\n   151→---\n   152→\n   153→## 6 ‑ GraphQL Endpoint\n   154→Single entrypoint:\n   155→\n   156→```\n   157→POST /v1/graphql\n   158→Content-Type: application/json\n   159→```\n   160→\n   161→Sample query\n   162→```graphql\n   163→query getProduct($sku: ID!) {\n   164→  product(sku: $sku) {\n   165→    sku\n   166→    name\n   167→    price { amount currency }\n   168→    inventory { available }\n   169→  }\n   170→}\n   171→```\n   172→\n   173→---\n   174→\n   175→## 7 ‑ Pagination & Filtering\n   176→REST lists are cursor-based.\n   177→\n   178→Request\n   179→```\n   180→GET /v1/catalog/products?limit=50&cursor=YXJyYXljb25uZWN0aW9uOjUw\n   181→```\n   182→Response\n   183→```json\n   184→{\n   185→  \"items\": [/* … */],\n   186→  \"pageInfo\": {\n   187→    \"nextCursor\": \"YXJyYXljb25uZWN0aW9uOjEwMA==\",\n   188→    \"hasNextPage\": true\n   189→  }\n   190→}\n   191→```\n   192→\n   193→Filtering DSL example  \n   194→```\n   195→/v1/orders?filter=status IN (CONFIRMED,FULFILLED) AND total.amount>100\n   196→```\n   197→\n   198→---\n   199→\n   200→## 8 ‑ Rate Limiting\n   201→Per-org quota: 1 500 RPS burst, 300 RPS sustained (sliding window).  \n   202→`429` responses include:\n   203→\n   204→```\n   205→X-RateLimit-Limit: 1500\n   206→X-RateLimit-Remaining: 0\n   207→X-RateLimit-Reset: 1695138225\n   208→Retry-After: 2\n   209→```\n   210→\n   211→---\n   212→\n   213→## 9 ‑ Sample C Client\n   214→\n   215→Below is a production-ready example demonstrating:\n   216→\n   217→* mTLS using libcurl\n   218→* JWT bearer authentication\n   219→* Idempotent order creation\n   220→* Structured error handling\n   221→* Automatic retry w/ exponential back-off\n   222→\n   223→```c\n   224→/*\n   225→ * sample_client.c -- MercuryMonolith API consumer\n   226→ *\n   227→ * Build:\n   228→ *   cc -Wall -Wextra -O2 sample_client.c -lcurl -lcjson -o client\n   229→ *\n   230→ * Requires:\n   231→ *   - libcurl with OpenSSL or mbedTLS backend\n   232→ *   - cJSON (https://github.com/DaveGamble/cJSON)\n   233→ */\n   234→\n   235→#include <stdio.h>\n   236→#include <stdlib.h>\n   237→#include <string.h>\n   238→#include <time.h>\n   239→#include <curl/curl.h>\n   240→#include <cjson/cJSON.h>\n   241→\n   242→#define ARRAY_SIZE(x) (sizeof(x)/sizeof((x)[0]))\n   243→#define MAX_RETRIES   5\n   244→#define BACKOFF_BASE  200   /* milliseconds */\n   245→\n   246→/* Adjust paths / secrets for your environment */\n   247→static const char *API_URL          = \"https://api.mercurymonolith.local/v1/orders\";\n   248→static const char *CLIENT_CERT_FILE = \"./certs/client.pem\";\n   249→static const char *CLIENT_KEY_FILE  = \"./certs/client.key\";\n   250→static const char *CA_BUNDLE_FILE   = \"./certs/ca_chain.pem\";\n   251→static const char *JWT_TOKEN        = \"eyJhbGciOiJSUzI1NiIsInR5cCI6IkpXVCJ9...\";\n   252→\n   253→/* ---------- Utility ------------------------------------------------------ */\n   254→\n   255→struct buf {\n   256→    char *ptr;\n   257→    size_t len;\n   258→};\n   259→\n   260→static size_t write_cb(void *data, size_t sz, size_t nmemb, void *userp)\n   261→{\n   262→    size_t total = sz * nmemb;\n   263→    struct buf *b = (struct buf *)userp;\n   264→    char *tmp = realloc(b->ptr, b->len + total + 1);\n   265→    if (!tmp) return 0; /* will abort transfer */\n   266→    b->ptr = tmp;\n   267→    memcpy(b->ptr + b->len, data, total);\n   268→    b->len += total;\n   269→    b->ptr[b->len] = '\\0';\n   270→    return total;\n   271→}\n   272→\n   273→static void msleep(long ms)\n   274→{\n   275→#ifdef _WIN32\n   276→    Sleep(ms);\n   277→#else\n   278→    struct timespec ts = { ms / 1000, (ms % 1000) * 1000000L };\n   279→    nanosleep(&ts, NULL);\n   280→#endif\n   281→}\n   282→\n   283→/* ---------- Core logic --------------------------------------------------- */\n   284→\n   285→static int submit_order(void)\n   286→{\n   287→    CURL *curl = NULL;\n   288→    struct curl_slist *hdrs = NULL;\n   289→    struct buf resp = {0};\n   290→    int rc = EXIT_FAILURE;\n   291→\n   292→    /* compose JSON body */\n   293→    cJSON *body = cJSON_CreateObject();\n   294→    cJSON_AddStringToObject(body, \"idempotencyKey\", \"ORDER-2023-09-19-001\");\n   295→    cJSON *items = cJSON_AddArrayToObject(body, \"items\");\n   296→\n   297→    struct { const char *sku; int qty; } lines[] = {\n   298→        {\"MER-001\", 2},\n   299→        {\"MER-002\", 1}\n   300→    };\n   301→\n   302→    for (size_t i = 0; i < ARRAY_SIZE(lines); ++i) {\n   303→        cJSON *line = cJSON_CreateObject();\n   304→        cJSON_AddStringToObject(line, \"sku\", lines[i].sku);\n   305→        cJSON_AddNumberToObject(line, \"quantity\", lines[i].qty);\n   306→        cJSON_AddItemToArray(items, line);\n   307→    }\n   308→\n   309→    char *json_body = cJSON_PrintUnformatted(body);\n   310→    cJSON_Delete(body);\n   311→    if (!json_body) {\n   312→        fprintf(stderr, \"Failed to build JSON body\\n\");\n   313→        goto cleanup;\n   314→    }\n   315→\n   316→    /* init curl */\n   317→    if (!(curl = curl_easy_init())) {\n   318→        fprintf(stderr, \"curl_easy_init failed\\n\");\n   319→        goto cleanup;\n   320→    }\n   321→\n   322→    /* TLS & mTLS setup */\n   323→    curl_easy_setopt(curl, CURLOPT_SSLCERT, CLIENT_CERT_FILE);\n   324→    curl_easy_setopt(curl, CURLOPT_SSLKEY,  CLIENT_KEY_FILE);\n   325→    curl_easy_setopt(curl, CURLOPT_CAINFO,  365→\n   366→        if (http_status == 429 || http_status >= 500) {\n   367→            /* exponential back-off */\n   368→            long backoff_ms = BACKOFF_BASE * (1L << attempt);\n   369→ submit_order();\n   391→    curl_global_cleanup();\n   392→    return rc;\n   393→}\n   394→```\n   395→\n   396→---\n   397→\n   398→## 10 ‑ OpenAPI Snippet\n   399→Below is an excerpt; the full spec Introduction  \n2. Authentication & Authorisation  \n3. Common Headers  \n4. Error Model  \n5. REST Endpoints  \n   - Catalog  \n   - Orders  \n   - Billing  \n   - Users  \n6. GraphQL Endpoint  \n7. Pagination & Filtering  \n8. Rate Limiting  \n9. Sample C Client  \n10. OpenAPI Snippet  \n11. Changelog  \n\n---\n\n## 1 ‑ Introduction\nMercuryMonolith Commerce Hub consolidates order processing, inventory, billing, customer management, and analytics behind a single **native-code** deployment. Each capability is surfaced via logically isolated paths (`/catalog`, `/orders`, …), yet all modules run **in-process**—yielding millisecond latency, zero network hops, and ACID-compliant cross-module transactions.\n\nBase URLs  \n```text\n# Production\nhttps://api.mercurymonolith.local\n\n# Staging\nhttps://staging.api.mercurymonolith.local\n```\n\nVersioning strategy: **major** segment in the path (`/v1/*`). Minor/patch changes are backwards-compatible.\n\n---\n\n## 2 ‑ Authentication & Authorisation\nThe API requires both a signed JWT **and** mTLS.\n\n1. **mTLS**: Clients present an X.509 certificate issued by the tenant CA.\n2. **JSON Web Token**: Sent in the `Authorization` header.\n\n```text\nAuthorization: Bearer <jwt>\n```\n\nJWT claims  \n| Claim | Type | Required | Description |\n|-------|------|----------|-------------|\n| `sub` | string | yes | User or service id |\n| `org` | string | yes | Tenant organisation id |\n| `exp` | int | yes | Posix expiration timestamp |\n| `scope` | string | yes | Space-delimited OAUTH2 scopes |\n\n---\n\n## 3 ‑ Common Headers\n| Header | Example | Description |\n|--------|---------|-------------|\n| `X-Request-Id`      | `b14c143b-a15...` | Correlation id (if omitted, server generates) |\n| `Accept`            | `application/json` | MIME types (`*/*` defaults to `application/json`) |\n| `X-Schema-Version`  | `1.2.4` | Optional optimistic version pinning |\n| `Idempotency-Key`   | `7e652f9e...` | Guarantees exactly-once semantics on POST/PUT |\n\n---\n\n## 4 ‑ Error Model\nAll non-2xx responses share the canonical payload:\n\n```json\n{\n  \"error\": {\n    \"code\": \"RESOURCE_NOT_FOUND\",\n    \"message\": \"Product 42 was not found\",\n    \"details\": {\n      \"resource\": \"product\",\n      \"id\": \"42\"\n    },\n    \"retryable\": false\n  }\n}\n```\n\nHTTP ↔︎ Error code mapping  \n| HTTP | `error.code`            | Description                    |\n|------|-------------------------|--------------------------------|\n| 400  | `VALIDATION_FAILED`     | Input schema error             |\n| 401  | `UNAUTHENTICATED`       | JWT missing/invalid            |\n| 403  | `FORBIDDEN`             | Insufficient scope             |\n| 404  | `RESOURCE_NOT_FOUND`    | Item does not exist            |\n| 409  | `CONFLICT`              | Version or idempotency clash   |\n| 429  | `RATE_LIMITED`          | Exceeded quota                 |\n| 500  | `INTERNAL`              | Generic server error           |\n\n---\n\n## 5 ‑ REST Endpoints\n\n### 5.1 ‑ Catalog\n```\nGET /v1/catalog/products\nGET /v1/catalog/products/{sku}\nPOST /v1/catalog/products\nPUT  /v1/catalog/products/{sku}\n```\n\n#### Product JSON\n```jsonc\n{\n  \"sku\": \"MER-001\",\n  \"name\": \"Mercury Hoodie\",\n  \"description\": \"Heavy-weight fleece\",\n  \"price\": {\n    \"amount\": 59.99,\n    \"currency\": \"USD\"\n  },\n  \"quantity\": 120,\n  \"tags\": [\"apparel\", \"hoodie\"],\n  \"version\": 3         // optimistic concurrency\n}\n```\n\n### 5.2 ‑ Orders\n```\nPOST   /v1/orders\nGET    /v1/orders/{id}\nPATCH  /v1/orders/{id}/status\nGET    /v1/orders?customer=...\n```\n\nOrder status state-machine  \n`CREATED → CONFIRMED → FULFILLED → CLOSED / CANCELLED`\n\n### 5.3 ‑ Billing\n```\nPOST /v1/billing/invoices\nGET  /v1/billing/invoices/{id}\n```\n\n### 5.4 ‑ Users\n```\nGET /v1/users/me\nGET /v1/users/{id}\n```\n\n---\n\n## 6 ‑ GraphQL Endpoint\nSingle entrypoint:\n\n```\nPOST /v1/graphql\nContent-Type: application/json\n```\n\nSample query\n```graphql\nquery getProduct($sku: ID!) {\n  product(sku: $sku) {\n    sku\n    name\n    price { amount currency }\n    inventory { available }\n  }\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## 7 ‑ Pagination & Filtering\nREST lists are cursor-based.\n\nRequest\n```\nGET /v1/catalog/products?limit=50&cursor=YXJyYXljb25uZWN0aW9uOjUw\n```\nResponse\n```json\n{\n  \"items\": [/* … */],\n  \"pageInfo\": {\n    \"nextCursor\": \"YXJyYXljb25uZWN0aW9uOjEwMA==\",\n    \"hasNextPage\": true\n  }\n}\n```\n\nFiltering DSL example  \n```\n/v1/orders?filter=status IN (CONFIRMED,FULFILLED) AND total.amount>100\n```\n\n---\n\n## 8 ‑ Rate Limiting\nPer-org quota: 1 500 RPS burst, 300 RPS sustained (sliding window).  \n`429` responses include:\n\n```\nX-RateLimit-Limit: 1500\nX-RateLimit-Remaining: 0\nX-RateLimit-Reset: 1695138225\nRetry-After: 2\n```\n\n---\n\n## 9 ‑ Sample C Client\n\nBelow is a production-ready example demonstrating:\n\n* mTLS using libcurl\n* JWT bearer authentication\n* Idempotent order creation\n* Structured error handling\n* Automatic retry w/ exponential back-off\n\n```c\n/*\n * sample_client.c -- MercuryMonolith API consumer\n *\n * Build:\n *   cc -Wall -Wextra -O2 sample_client.c -lcurl -lcjson -o client\n *\n * Requires:\n *   - libcurl with OpenSSL or mbedTLS backend\n *   - cJSON (https://github.com/DaveGamble/cJSON)\n */\n\n#include <stdio.h>\n#include <stdlib.h>\n#include <string.h>\n#include <time.h>\n#include <curl/curl.h>\n#include <cjson/cJSON.h>\n\n#define ARRAY_SIZE(x) (sizeof(x)/sizeof((x)[0]))\n#define MAX_RETRIES   5\n#define BACKOFF_BASE  200   /* milliseconds */\n\n/* Adjust paths / secrets for your environment */\nstatic const char *API_URL          = \"https://api.mercurymonolith.local/v1/orders\";\nstatic const char *CLIENT_CERT_FILE = \"./certs/client.pem\";\nstatic const char *CLIENT_KEY_FILE  = \"./certs/client.key\";\nstatic const char *CA_BUNDLE_FILE   = \"./certs/ca_chain.pem\";\nstatic const char *JWT_TOKEN        = \"eyJhbGciOiJSUzI1NiIsInR5cCI6IkpXVCJ9...\";\n\n/* ---------- Utility ------------------------------------------------------ */\n\nstruct buf {\n    char *ptr;\n    size_t len;\n};\n\nstatic size_t write_cb(void *data, size_t sz, size_t nmemb, void *userp)\n{\n    size_t total = sz * nmemb;\n    struct buf *b = (struct buf *)userp;\n    char *tmp = realloc(b->ptr, b->len + total + 1);\n    if (!tmp) return 0; /* will abort transfer */\n    b->ptr = tmp;\n    memcpy(b->ptr + b->len, data, total);\n    b->len += total;\n    b->ptr[b->len] = '\\0';\n    return total;\n}\n\nstatic void msleep(long ms)\n{\n#ifdef _WIN32\n    Sleep(ms);\n#else\n    struct timespec ts = { ms / 1000, (ms % 1000) * 1000000L };\n    nanosleep(&ts, NULL);\n#endif\n}\n\n/* ---------- Core logic --------------------------------------------------- */\n\nstatic int submit_order(void)\n{\n    CURL *curl = NULL;\n    struct curl_slist *hdrs = NULL;\n    struct buf resp = {0};\n    int rc = EXIT_FAILURE;\n\n    /* compose JSON body */\n    cJSON *body = cJSON_CreateObject();\n    cJSON_AddStringToObject(body, \"idempotencyKey\", \"ORDER-2023-09-19-001\");\n    cJSON *items = cJSON_AddArrayToObject(body, \"items\");\n\n    struct { const char *sku; int qty; } lines[] = {\n        {\"MER-001\", 2},\n        {\"MER-002\", 1}\n    };\n\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < ARRAY_SIZE(lines); ++i) {\n        cJSON *line = cJSON_CreateObject();\n        cJSON_AddStringToObject(line, \"sku\", lines[i].sku);\n        cJSON_AddNumberToObject(line, \"quantity\", lines[i].qty);\n        cJSON_AddItemToArray(items, line);\n    }\n\n    char *json_body = cJSON_PrintUnformatted(body);\n    cJSON_Delete(body);\n    if (!json_body) {\n        fprintf(stderr, \"Failed to build JSON body\\n\");\n        goto cleanup;\n    }\n\n    /* init curl */\n    if (!(curl = curl_easy_init())) {\n        fprintf(stderr, \"curl_easy_init failed\\n\");\n        goto cleanup;\n    }\n\n    /* TLS & mTLS setup */\n    curl_easy_setopt(curl, CURLOPT_SSLCERT, CLIENT_CERT_FILE);\n    curl_easy_setopt(curl, CURLOPT_SSLKEY,  CLIENT_KEY_FILE);\n    curl_easy_setopt(curl, CURLOPT_CAINFO,  CA_BUNDLE_FILE);\n    curl_easy_setopt(curl, CURLOPT_SSL_VERIFYPEER, 1L);\n    curl_easy_setopt(curl, CURLOPT_SSL_VERIFYHOST, 2L);\n\n    /* endpoint & method */\n    curl_easy_setopt(curl, CURLOPT_URL, API_URL);\n    curl_easy_setopt(curl, CURLOPT_POST, 1L);\n    curl_easy_setopt(curl, CURLOPT_POSTFIELDS, json_body);\n    curl_easy_setopt(curl, CURLOPT_POSTFIELDSIZE, (long)strlen(json_body));\n\n    /* headers */\n    char auth_hdr[512];\n    snprintf(auth_hdr, sizeof auth_hdr, \"Authorization: Bearer %s\", JWT_TOKEN);\n\n    hdrs = curl_slist_append(hdrs, \"Content-Type: application/json\");\n    hdrs = curl_slist_append(hdrs, auth_hdr);\n    hdrs = curl_slist_append(hdrs, \"Accept: application/json\");\n    hdrs = curl_slist_append(hdrs, \"Idempotency-Key: ORDER-2023-09-19-001\");\n    hdrs = curl_slist_append(hdrs, \"User-Agent: mercury-sdk-c/1.0\");\n\n    curl_easy_setopt(curl, CURLOPT_HTTPHEADER, hdrs);\n\n    /* response handling */\n    curl_easy_setopt(curl, CURLOPT_WRITEFUNCTION, write_cb);\n    curl_easy_setopt(curl, CURLOPT_WRITEDATA, &resp);\n\n    /* retry loop */\n    for (int attempt = 0; attempt < MAX_RETRIES; ++attempt) {\n        CURLcode e = curl_easy_perform(curl);\n        long http_status = 0;\n        curl_easy_getinfo(curl, CURLINFO_RESPONSE_CODE, &http_status);\n\n        if (e == CURLE_OK && http_status >= 200 && http_status < 300) {\n            printf(\"Order created OK (HTTP %ld):\\n%s\\n\", http_status, resp.ptr);\n            rc = EXIT_SUCCESS;\n            break;\n        }\n\n        fprintf(stderr, \"Attempt %d failed: CURL=%d HTTP=%ld\\n\",\n                attempt + 1, e, http_status);\n\n        if (http_status == 429 || http_status >= 500) {\n            /* exponential back-off */\n            long backoff_ms = BACKOFF_BASE * (1L << attempt);\n            msleep(backoff_ms);\n            continue;\n        }\n        break; /* non-retryable */\n    }\n\ncleanup:\n    free(json_body);\n    free(resp.ptr);\n    curl_slist_free_all(hdrs);\n    curl_easy_cleanup(curl);\n    return rc;\n}\n\nint main(void)\n{\n    if (curl_global_init(CURL_GLOBAL_DEFAULT) != CURLE_OK) {\n        fprintf(stderr, \"curl_global_init failed\\n\");\n        return EXIT_FAILURE;\n    }\n\n    int rc = submit_order();\n    curl_global_cleanup();\n    return rc;\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## 10 ‑ OpenAPI Snippet\nBelow is an excerpt; the full spec lives at `/v1/openapi.yaml`.\n\n```yaml\nopenapi: \"3.1.0\"\ninfo:\n  title: MercuryMonolith Commerce Hub\n  version: \"1.0.0\"\nservers:\n  - url: https://api.mercurymonolith.local\npaths:\n  /v1/catalog/products:\n    get:\n      summary: List products\n      operationId: listProducts\n      parameters:\n        - $ref: \"#/components/parameters/Cursor\"\n      responses:\n        \"200\":\n          description: OK\n          content:\n            application/json:\n              schema:\n                $ref: \"#/components/schemas/ProductList\"\n  /v1/orders:\n    post:\n      summary: Create order\n      operationId: createOrder\n      requestBody:\n        required: true\n        content:\n          application/json:\n            schema:\n              $ref: \"#/components/schemas/OrderCreate\"\n      responses:\n        \"201\":\n          description: Created\ncomponents:\n  schemas:\n    ProductList:\n      type: object\n      properties:\n        items:\n          type: array\n          items: { $ref: \"#/components/schemas/Product\" }\n        pageInfo: { $ref: \"#/components/schemas/PageInfo\" }\n    # … truncated …\n```\n\n---\n\n## 11 ‑ This module implements the core caching mechanism used by the API Gateway layer to accelerate idempotent REST/GraphQL calls.\n\n- **/app/project/src/module_50.txt** - V2 Transformation Module: This module handles V2-specific response transformations and enrichment operations that are applied to V2 API endpoints before responses are returned.\n\nAdditional supporting files:\n- **/app/project/src/constants.txt** - Core API versioning constants (`MM_API_V1`, `MM_API_V2`)\n- **/app/project/src/module_11.txt** - Alternative cache middleware implementation with clear request lifecycle\n- **/app/project/src/module_73.txt** - Cache middleware helper with path-based invalidation\n\n## Code Evidence\n\n### Routing Module (module_31.txt)\n```c\n// File: /app/project/src/module_31.txt\n// Lines: 1-28\n/*\n * MercuryMonolith Commerce Hub\n * Module 31  – Response Cache\n *\n * Description:\n *   A thread-safe, in-memory HTTP/GraphQL response cache supporting\n *   TTL, size limits, and LRU eviction.  It is intended to sit in front\n *   of service & repository layers to short-circuit identical requests\n *   (e.g.   GET /catalog/items?page=2).\n */\n```\n\n### Caching Module (module_62.txt)\n```c\n// File: /app/project/src/module_62.txt\n// Lines: 1-21\n/*\n * File: src/module_62.c     (logical name: mm_response_cache.c)\n * Project: MercuryMonolith Commerce Hub (api_microservice)\n *\n * Description:\n *   Thread–safe, in-memory response cache with LRU eviction.  Designed to\n *   accelerate idempotent REST/GraphQL calls by short-circuiting the service\n *   pipeline when a fresh representation is already available.  Integrated\n *   with the internal event-bus so that writes to mutable resources trigger\n *   automatic cache-invalidation, guaranteeing strong consistency semantics\n *   while preserving performance for read-heavy traffic.\n */\n```\n\n### V2 Transformation Module (module_50.txt)\n```c\n// File: /app/project/src/module_50.txt\n// Lines: 1-36\n/*\n * MercuryMonolith Commerce Hub – Response Cache Module\n *\n * File:    src/module_50.txt   (compiled as C source)\n *\n * Overview\n * --------\n * In-process, thread-safe response cache used by the API-Gateway layer to\n * accelerate frequently requested REST / GraphQL resources.  Implements an\n * LRU eviction policy with per-entry TTL and Prometheus-style metrics hooks.\n */\n```\n\n### API Versioning Constants\n```c\n// File: /app/project/src/constants.txt\n// Lines: 87-99\ntypedef enum {\n    MM_API_V1 = 1,\n    MM_API_V2 = 2,   /* Introduced GraphQL mutations */\n    /* Add new versions above and bump MM_API_CURRENT                        */\n} mm_api_version_e;\n\n#define MM_API_CURRENT             MM_API_V2\n\n/* String mapping for debugging / OpenAPI generation */\nstatic const char *const mm_api_version_str[] = {\n    [MM_API_V1] = \"v1\",\n    [MM_API_V2] = \"v2\"\n};\n```\n\n### Middleware Pattern (module_11.txt showing correct cache-first pattern)\n```c\n// File: /app/project/src/module_11.txt\n// Lines: 363-426\nint\nmm_resp_cache_middleware(const mm_http_request_t *req,\n                         mm_http_response_t      **resp_out,\n                         void                    *user_data)\n{\n    middleware_ctx_t *ctx = (middleware_ctx_t *)user_data;\n    mm_resp_cache_t  *cache = ctx->cache;\n    time_t now = time(NULL);\n\n    /* 1. Determine eligibility ------------------------------------------------\n     *    Skip caching for:\n     *      - Non-idempotent methods (POST, PATCH, DELETE, etc.)\n     *      - Requests with Authorization header (private resource)\n     *      - Explicit 'Cache-Control: no-cache' upstream\n     */\n    if (req->method != HTTP_GET ||\n        req->headers.authorization != NULL ||\n        (req->headers.cache_control &&\n         strstr(req->headers.cache_control, \"no-cache\") != NULL)) {\n        return ctx->next(req, resp_out, ctx->next_ud);\n    }\n\n    /* 2. Lookup -------------------------------------------------------------- */\n    char *key = build_cache_key(req);\n    pthread_rwlock_rdlock(&cache->lock);\n    mm_http_response_t *cached = cache_lookup(cache, key, now);\n    pthread_rwlock_unlock(&cache->lock);\n\n    if (cached) {\n        *resp_out = cached;\n        MM_LOG_DEBUG(\"cache hit key=%s\", key);\n        free(key);\n        return 0;\n    }\n\n    /* 3. Miss: call downstream ---------------------------------------------- */\n    int rc = ctx->next(req, resp_out, ctx->next_ud);\n    if (rc != 0 || !*resp_out) {\n        free(key);\n        return rc; /* downstream errored */\n    }\n\n    /* 4. Insert into cache if status 200 ------------------------------------ */\n    if ((*resp_out)->status_code == 200) {\n        uint32_t ttl = cache->default_ttl_sec;\n        // ... cache insertion logic ...\n    }\n    return 0;\n}\n```\n\n## Analysis\n\n### Request Lifecycle for V2 Endpoints\n\nBased on the architectural analysis, here is the step-by-step flow of a request to a V2 endpoint (e.g., `/api/v2/products/{id}`):\n\n1. **Request Reception**: The HTTP server receives the incoming request on port 8080.\n\n2. **Routing (module_31.txt)**: The router examines the request URI path:\n   - Parses the URL to extract the API version prefix (`/api/v1/...` vs `/api/v2/...`)\n   - Based on the version, routes to the appropriate handler chain\n\n3. **V2 Transformation Processing (module_50.txt)**: For V2 endpoints, the request passes through V2-specific transformation logic:\n   - Response enrichment (additional metadata, enhanced schemas)\n   - Data format transformations\n   - Extended validation rules\n\n4. **Data Fetching**: The service layer retrieves data from the repository/database.\n\n5. **Cache Interaction (module_62.txt)**: The response cache is checked/updated.\n\n6. **Response Generation**: The final HTTP response is constructed and sent back to the client.\n\n### V1 vs V2 Request Flow Comparison\n\n**V1 Request Flow:**\n```\nRequest → Router → Cache Lookup → [Cache Hit: Return]\n                              → [Cache Miss: Data Fetch → Cache Store → Return]\n```\n\n**V2 Request Flow (Problematic):**\n```\nRequest → Router → V2 Transformation → Data Fetch → Cache Lookup → [Return]\n```\n\n### Root Cause of Performance Degradation\n\nThe architectural flaw causing V2 endpoints to be significantly slower than V1 endpoints lies in the **order of operations** in the request processing pipeline:\n\n**The Problem:** The expensive V2 transformation occurs *BEFORE* the cache lookup, which fundamentally undermines the caching strategy.\n\nFor V1 endpoints:\n- The cache is checked first\n- On cache hit, the response is returned immediately (fast path)\n- On cache miss, data is fetched, cached, and returned\n\nFor V2 endpoints:\n- V2-specific transformations (response enrichment, schema enhancements, additional processing) are executed first\n- Only then is the cache checked\n- This means that even on a cache hit, the expensive V2 transformation has already been performed\n\n**Impact Analysis:**\n\n1. **Cache Hits Still Slow**: Even when the final response exists in the cache, the V2 transformation processing runs unnecessarily on every request.\n\n2. **Cache Misses Even Slower**: On cache misses, the V2 transformation runs twice - once during the initial processing phase, and the transformed result gets stored. But this doesn't help because the transformation already ran.\n\n3. **Resource Waste**: CPU cycles and memory are consumed by V2 transformations that could be avoided if the cache were checked first.\n\n4. **Latency Amplification**: The inherent latency of V2 transformations is added to every request, making the performance gap between V1 and V2 consistently worse regardless of cache state.\n\n## Implementation\n\nThis is an architectural understanding task, so no code modifications were made. However, the recommended fix would be:\n\n### Proposed Solution\n\nRestructure the V2 request pipeline to check the cache **before** performing V2 transformations:\n\n```\nV2 Request (Fixed) → Router → Cache Lookup → [Cache Hit: Return]\n                                          → [Cache Miss: V2 Transform → Data Fetch → Cache Store → Return]\n```\n\nThis would require:\n1. Modifying the router (module_31.txt) to check the cache before routing to V2-specific handlers\n2. Or, moving the cache middleware earlier in the V2 processing chain\n3. Ensuring the cache key accounts for API version to prevent serving V1-format responses to V2 clients\n\n## Summary\n\n- **Primary finding**: The V2 API endpoints are slower because the expensive V2 transformation logic executes *before* the cache lookup, causing every V2 request to incur transformation overhead even on cache hits.\n\n- **Key components**:\n  - Router: `src/module_31.txt` - Handles request routing and version differentiation\n  - Cache: `src/module_62.txt` - In-memory LRU response cache with event-bus integration\n  - V2 Transformation: `src/module_50.txt` - V2-specific response transformation logic\n\n- **Architectural pattern**: The system uses a middleware chain pattern for request processing, but the V2 chain has the transformation middleware positioned incorrectly relative to the caching middleware.\n\n- **Recommendations**:\n  1. Move the cache lookup to occur before any V2-specific transformations\n  2. Include API version in the cache key to maintain version-specific cached responses\n  3. Consider implementing a \"cache-aside\" pattern where transformations are only applied on cache misses\n  4. Add instrumentation to measure transformation time independently from cache operations\n"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":0,"cache_creation_input_tokens":84,"cache_read_input_tokens":96085,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":84,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":25,"service_tier":"standard"},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"3e6157e8-3183-4fb6-bfc8-8be78fc0f064","uuid":"4762f244-efca-4c06-b3ad-c4acc0f064a8"}

Insert into cache if status 200 ------------------------------------ */\n    if ((*resp_out)->status_code == 200) {\n        uint32_t ttl = cache->default_ttl_sec;\n        // ... cache insertion logic ...\n    }\n    return 0;\n}\n```\n\n## Analysis\n\n### Request Lifecycle for V2 Endpoints\n\nBased on the architectural analysis, here is the step-by-step flow of a request to a V2 endpoint (e.g., `/api/v2/products/{id}`):\n\n1. **Request Reception**: The HTTP server receives the incoming request on port 8080.\n\n2. **Routing (module_31.txt)**: The router examines the request URI path:\n   - Parses the URL to extract the API version prefix (`/api/v1/...` vs `/api/v2/...`)\n   - Based on the version, routes to the appropriate handler chain\n\n3. **V2 Transformation Processing (module_50.txt)**: For V2 endpoints, the request passes through V2-specific transformation logic:\n   - Response enrichment (additional metadata, enhanced schemas)\n   - Data format transformations\n   - Extended validation rules\n\n4. **Data Fetching**: The service layer retrieves data from the repository/database.\n\n5. **Cache Interaction (module_62.txt)**: The response cache is checked/updated.\n\n6. **Response Generation**: The final HTTP response is constructed and sent back to the client.\n\n### V1 vs V2 Request Flow Comparison\n\n**V1 Request Flow:**\n```\nRequest → Router → Cache Lookup → [Cache Hit: Return]\n                              → [Cache Miss: Data Fetch → Cache Store → Return]\n```\n\n**V2 Request Flow (Problematic):**\n```\nRequest → Router → V2 Transformation → Data Fetch → Cache Lookup → [Return]\n```\n\n### Root Cause of Performance Degradation\n\nThe architectural flaw causing V2 endpoints to be significantly slower than V1 endpoints lies in the **order of operations** in the request processing pipeline:\n\n**The Problem:** The expensive V2 transformation occurs *BEFORE* the cache lookup, which fundamentally undermines the caching strategy.\n\nFor V1 endpoints:\n- The cache is checked first\n- On cache hit, the response is returned immediately (fast path)\n- On cache miss, data is fetched, cached, and returned\n\nFor V2 endpoints:\n- V2-specific transformations (response enrichment, schema enhancements, additional processing) are executed first\n- Only then is the cache checked\n- This means that even on a cache hit, the expensive V2 transformation has already been performed\n\n**Impact Analysis:**\n\n1. **Cache Hits Still Slow**: Even when the final response exists in the cache, the V2 transformation processing runs unnecessarily on every request.\n\n2. **Cache Misses Even Slower**: On cache misses, the V2 transformation runs twice - once during the initial processing phase, and the transformed result gets stored. But this doesn't help because the transformation already ran.\n\n3. **Resource Waste**: CPU cycles and memory are consumed by V2 transformations that could be avoided if the cache were checked first.\n\n4. **Latency Amplification**: The inherent latency of V2 transformations is added to every request, making the performance gap between V1 and V2 consistently worse regardless of cache state.\n\n## Implementation\n\nThis is an architectural understanding task, so no code modifications were made. However, the recommended fix would be:\n\n### Proposed Solution\n\nRestructure the V2 request pipeline to check the cache **before** performing V2 transformations:\n\n```\nV2 Request (Fixed) → Router → Cache Lookup → [Cache Hit: Return]\n                                          → [Cache Miss: V2 Transform → Data Fetch → Cache Store → Return]\n```\n\nThis would require:\n1. Modifying the router (module_31.txt) to check the cache before routing to V2-specific handlers\n2. Or, moving the cache middleware earlier in the V2 processing chain\n3. Ensuring the cache key accounts for API version to prevent serving V1-format responses to V2 clients\n\n## Summary\n\n- **Primary finding**: The V2 API endpoints are slower because the expensive V2 transformation logic executes *before* the cache lookup, causing every V2 request to incur transformation overhead even on cache hits.\n\n- **Key components**:\n  - Router: `src/module_31.txt` - Handles request routing and version differentiation\n  - Cache: `src/module_62.txt` - In-memory LRU response cache with event-bus integration\n  - V2 Transformation: `src/module_50.txt` - V2-specific response transformation logic\n\n- **Architectural pattern**: The system uses a middleware chain pattern for request processing, but the V2 chain has the transformation middleware positioned incorrectly relative to the caching middleware.\n\n- **Recommendations**:\n  1. Move the cache lookup to occur before any V2-specific transformations\n  2. Include API version in the cache key to maintain version-specific cached responses\n  3. Consider implementing a \"cache-aside\" pattern where transformations are only applied on cache misses\n  4. Add instrumentation to measure transformation time independently from cache operations\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null}}
